### [37] [LUCID: Learning Unified Control for Image Deflaring and Exposure Mastery in Nighttime Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06901)
*Tingyu Yang,Yuan Cheng,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUCID是一个统一框架，通过flare解缠模块和扩散驱动模块将夜间图像恢复重新定义为一个连续、可控的过程，支持用户通过分类器自由引导（CFG）选择性地控制光源及其相关伪影，并实现高动态范围（HDR）重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将夜间场景中的强眩光和光子受限区域噪声视为孤立退化进行处理，忽略了它们本质上的相互纠缠关系。

Method: 提出两阶段协作框架：①flare解缠模块移除光学伪影以提供结构指导；②扩散驱动模块利用生成先验重建干净、曝光良好的图像。引入四模式训练策略实现显式可控性，通过CFG允许用户选择性控制光源及关联伪影，并支持连续曝光控制的HDR重建。

Result: 在多种真实世界夜间场景上，LUCID consistently优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LUCID通过将夜间恢复建模为连续可控过程而非固定校正，有效解决了传统方法忽略退化纠缠的问题，并在真实场景中取得最佳性能。

Abstract: Photography is the art of painting with light, yet nighttime scenes are shaped by competing degradations: intense flares obscure scene structure, while photon-limited regions collapse into noise. Conventional approaches address these factors in isolation, overlooking the fact that these degradations are fundamentally entangled. To bridge this gap, we introduce LUCID, a unified framework that reframes nighttime restoration as a continuous and controllable process rather than a fixed correction. We decompose nighttime restoration into two cooperative components: a flare disentanglement module that lifts the 'curtain' of optical artifacts to provide reliable structural guidance, and a diffusion-driven module that leverages generative priors to reconstruct clean and well-exposed imagery. Crucially, LUCID introduces explicit controllability through a novel four-mode training strategy, enabling users to steer the restoration process via classifier-free guidance (CFG) and allowing selective control over light sources and their associated flare and ghosting artifacts, while also supporting high dynamic range (HDR) reconstruction through continuous exposure control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LUCID consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse real-world nighttime scenarios.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond Skeletons: Learning Animation Directly from Driving Videos with Same2X Training Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06903)
*Yuan Zeng,Yujia Shi,Yuhao Yang,Dongxia Liu,Zongqing Lu,Wenming Yang,Qingmin Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DirectAnimator是一个直接利用原始驱动视频进行人体图像动画生成的框架，通过引入Driving Cue Triplet（姿态、面部、位置线索）和CueFusion DiT模块实现鲁棒控制，并采用Same2X训练策略对齐跨ID特征，在保持高视觉质量和身份一致性的同时减少计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖姿态估计器提取中间表示，但在遮挡或复杂姿态下容易出错。本文旨在绕过姿态提取步骤，直接从原始驱动视频中学习运动信息。

Method: 提出DirectAnimator框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Driving Cue Triplet（姿态、面部、位置三重线索）捕获运动、表情和对齐信息；(2) CueFusion DiT模块融合这些线索用于去噪控制；(3) Same2X训练策略对齐跨ID特征与同ID特征，加速收敛。

Result: 在多个基准上达到最先进的视觉质量和身份保持效果，对遮挡和复杂关节运动具有鲁棒性，且计算资源需求更低。

Conclusion: DirectAnimator通过直接学习驱动视频并融合多线索表示，有效克服了姿态估计器的局限性，实现了高效、鲁棒的人体动画生成。

Abstract: Human image animation aims to generate a video from a static reference image, guided by pose information extracted from a driving video. Existing approaches often rely on pose estimators to extract intermediate representations, but such signals are prone to errors under occlusion or complex poses. Building on these observations, we present DirectAnimator, a framework that bypasses pose extraction and directly learns from raw driving videos. We introduce a Driving Cue Triplet consisting of pose, face, and location cues that captures motion, expression, and alignment in a semantically rich yet stable form, and we fuse them through a CueFusion DiT block for reliable control during denoising. To make learning dependable when the driving and reference identities differ, we devise a Same2X training strategy that aligns cross-ID features with those learned from same-ID data, regularizing optimization and accelerating convergence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DirectAnimator attains state-of-the-art visual quality and identity preservation while remaining robust to occlusions and complex articulation, and it does so with fewer computational resources. Our project page is at https://directanimator.github.io/.

</details>


### [39] [polyDAG: Polynomial Acyclicity Constraints for Efficient Continuous Causal Discovery in Visual Semantic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06908)
*Wenhao Zhang,Ramin Ramezani,Tao Han,Kai Hwang,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: polyDAG提出了一种基于多项式无环性约束的连续因果发现框架，用于视觉语义图学习。它用有限多项式迹约束替代矩阵指数约束，并推导了几何级数实现来避免显式求和循环。在合成图和CelebA面部属性数据集上，polyDAG在效率和结构恢复方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像分析流程通常将图像转换为结构化的语义变量（如面部属性、物体概念、场景描述符），学习这些变量之间的有向依赖关系可以生成可解释的视觉语义图。然而，连续有向无环图学习受到无环性约束计算成本高的限制，现有方法使用矩阵指数约束，计算开销大，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: polyDAG提出了一个多项式无环性框架：1）用有限多项式迹约束替代矩阵指数约束，并证明新约束在无环图上恰好为零；2）推导了几何级数实现，避免了显式求和循环，同时保持相同的无环性条件。

Result: 在合成Erdos-Renyi图和CelebA面部视觉属性上的实验表明：在d∈{100,200,500}的合成协议上，平均结构汉明距离从318.4降至285.4，平均F1分数从0.725提升至0.756。在100个节点上，几何变体运行时间为3.44秒，相比指数基线的5.16秒，加速了33.4%。

Conclusion: polyDAG通过多项式无环性约束有效提升了连续因果发现中视觉语义图学习的效率和结构恢复质量，代码已公开。该方法为大规模因果图学习提供了一种计算上更经济的替代方案。

Abstract: Modern image-analysis pipelines often convert images into structured semantic variables, such as facial attributes, object concepts, and scene descriptors. Learning directed dependencies among these variables can produce interpretable visual semantic graphs, but continuous directed acyclic graph learning is limited by the cost of enforcing acyclicity. We present polyDAG, a polynomial acyclicity framework for efficient continuous causal discovery in visual semantic graphs. polyDAG replaces the matrix-exponential acyclicity constraint with a finite polynomial trace constraint and proves that the new constraint is zero exactly for acyclic graphs. We further derive a geometric-series implementation that avoids the explicit summation loop while preserving the same acyclicity condition. Experiments on synthetic Erdos-Renyi graphs and CelebA facial visual attributes show that polyDAG improves efficiency and structure recovery. Averaged over the revised synthetic protocol with d in {100, 200, 500}, polyDAG reduces mean structural Hamming distance from 318.4 to 285.4 and improves mean F1 score from 0.725 to 0.756. At 100 nodes, the geometric variant runs in 3.44 seconds compared with 5.16 seconds for the exponential baseline, corresponding to a 33.4 percent speedup. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/wenhaoz-fengcai/polyDAG.

</details>


### [40] [DRIFT: From Robustness Gaps to Invariance Manifolds for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06918)
*Abhishek Ameta,Sayan Banerjee,Shreyas Pandith,Harshit,Ankita Chatterjee,Akshay Janardan Bankar,Amit Satish Unde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于结构化不变性流形的AI生成图像检测方法，通过轻量级投影头将视觉基础模型表示分解为鲁棒和脆弱子空间，利用单类监督学习真实图像流形，实现跨生成器的开放世界泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于鲁棒性差距的检测方法依赖预训练的固定不变性几何结构，缺乏针对检测任务的自适应调整，在开放世界场景下泛化能力有限。

Method: 在冻结视觉基础模型上引入轻量级投影头，将表示空间分解为互补的鲁棒子空间（抑制物理变换）和脆弱子空间（保持对编辑类扰动敏感）。通过结构化排序间隔强制物理不变性与编辑诱导变异性之间的层次分离，将检测转化为相对于学习流形的间隔违反测试。推理时采用多尺度块级漂移生成双通道不变性签名和可解释定位图。

Result: 在跨生成器和分辨率的开放世界泛化实验中，该方法持续优于基于训练自由鲁棒性检测的基线方法，并提供可解释的不变性违反定位图。

Conclusion: 通过单类监督学习结构化不变性流形，实现了对未见生成器生成图像的有效检测，为开放世界AI生成图像检测提供了兼具强泛化性和可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative image models challenges existing AI-generated image detectors, particularly in open-world settings with unseen generators. Recent training-free approaches measure robustness gaps in frozen vision foundation models (VFMs), detecting fakes via perturbation-induced embedding drift. However, these methods rely on fixed invariance geometry inherited from pretraining and lack principled adaptation to the detection task. We instead formulate AI-generated image detection as learning a structured invariance manifold of real images under one-class supervision. Building upon a frozen VFM, we introduce lightweight projection heads that decompose representation space into complementary robust and fragile subspaces. The robust subspace is explicitly trained to suppress variations induced by physically plausible imaging transformations, approximating tangent directions of a real-image manifold, while the fragile subspace retains sensitivity to edit-like perturbations. A structured ordering margin enforces hierarchical separation between physical invariance and edit-induced variability, enabling detection as a margin-violation test relative to the learned manifold. At inference, multi-scale patch-wise drift under both transformation families yields a dual-channel invariance signature and interpretable localization. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong open-world generalization across unseen generators and resolutions, consistently outperforming training-free robustness-based baselines while providing interpretable invariance-violation maps.

</details>


### [41] [When CLIP Sees More, It Fights Back Harder: Multi-View Guided Adaptive Counterattacks for Test-Time Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06938)
*Sunoh Kim,Daeho Um*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAC通过多视图构建、感知腐败的自适应软加权对抗反击机制，在保持零样本推理效率的同时显著提升了CLIP模型对对抗扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时对抗反击(TTC)方法因依赖直接损坏的原始视图和使用无法适应不同损坏程度的噪声驱动硬门控机制，在强攻击下仍显脆弱。

Method: MAC首先构建输入图像的增强视图以获得多样化嵌入，然后对每个视图进行对抗反击以优化被破坏的嵌入，接着基于估计的腐败程度自适应缩放每个视图的反击强度，最后聚合自适应反击后的视图得到鲁棒预测。

Result: 在20个数据集和多种攻击场景下的广泛实验表明，MAC在显著提升鲁棒性的同时，凭借其免调优设计保持了高推理速度和内存效率。

Conclusion: MAC通过多视图感知腐败的自适应对抗反击机制，有效解决了TTC方法的局限性，实现了对抗鲁棒性与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP have achieved remarkable zero-shot recognition capabilities, yet their robustness against adversarial perturbations remains limited. Test-time counterattack (TTC) was recently proposed to improve CLIP's robustness by perturbing an input image to steer it away from a corrupted state during inference. However, TTC remains fragile under strong attacks because its counterattack relies on a directly corrupted original view and employs a noise-driven hard-gating scheme that cannot adapt to varying corruption severity. To address these limitations, we introduce Multi-view guided Adaptive Counterattack (MAC), which performs counterattacks for multi-view with corruption-aware soft weighting. Specifically, MAC first constructs augmented views of an input image to obtain diverse embeddings. It then performs counterattacks to refine corrupted embeddings of views. Next, MAC adaptively scales the counterattack intensity for each view based on its estimated corruption degree. Finally, the adaptively counterattacked views are aggregated to yield a robust final prediction. Extensive experiments across 20 datasets and diverse attack scenarios demonstrate that MAC substantially improves robustness while preserving high inference speed and memory efficiency with its tuning-free design. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunoh-kim/MAC.

</details>


### [42] [SS-TPT: Stability and Suitability-Guided Test-Time Prompt Tuning for Adversarially Robust Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06943)
*Sunoh Kim,Daeho Um*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SS-TPT方法，通过稳定性（预测对弱增强的不变性）和适宜性（视图间的特征空间密度）两种评分引导测试时提示微调，在保持高吞吐量的同时显著提升对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于测试时适应的VLM防御方法依赖大量增强视图，导致推理速度显著下降，存在鲁棒性与吞吐量之间的权衡问题。

Method: 设计双评分机制：稳定性评分衡量预测对弱增强的不变性，适宜性评分评估特征空间中的视图密度。结合SS引导的一致性损失和SS加权预测，在适应和推理阶段放大可信视图、抑制受损视图。

Result: 在多个数据集和不同视图数量下，SS-TPT在鲁棒性-吞吐量权衡上显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了其实用性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SS-TPT通过轻量级视图质量评估机制，实现了高效且鲁棒的测试时自适应，有效解决了现有防御方法的效率瓶颈。代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP achieve strong zero-shot recognition but remain highly fragile under adversarial perturbations. Recent test-time adaptation defenses improve robustness by leveraging many augmented views, but this leads to impractical slowdown and a clear robustness-throughput trade-off. To address this challenge, we present Stability and Suitability-guided Test-time Prompt Tuning (SS-TPT), evaluating the quality of each augmented view via two complementary scores: (1) stability, measuring prediction invariance to weak augmentations, and (2) suitability, measuring feature-space density among views. These stability and suitability (SS) scores guide both adaptation and inference through an SS-guided consistency loss and an SS-weighted prediction, amplifying trustworthy views while suppressing corrupted ones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SS-TPT significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior robustness-throughput trade-offs across diverse datasets and varying numbers of views, thereby demonstrating both strong practicality and generality. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunoh-kim/SS-TPT.

</details>


### [43] [When is 3D Worth It? A Resource-Performance Frontier for CNNs and Transformers in Lung CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06950)
*Md Enamul Hoq,Sharafat Hossain,Imraul Emmaka,Linda Larson-Prior,Lawrence Tarbox,Jonathan Bona,Donald Johann Jr. and Fred Prior*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了2D、2.5D和3D输入维度下CNN与ViT在肺结节分类任务中的表现，发现2.5D CNN在区分度与稳定性之间取得最佳平衡（ROC-AUC 0.682），而3D CNN存在阈值不稳定问题，Transformer出现退化预测。研究强调在类不平衡的肺癌筛查场景中，2D/2.5D输入比全3D表示提供更可靠的计算-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维模型被广泛认为对体积医学成像更优，但其实际价值取决于性能提升是否足以证明额外计算成本与复杂性的合理性。现有研究缺乏在固定训练协议下系统比较输入维度（2D、2.5D、3D）对模型行为影响的严格对照实验。

Method: 采用无泄漏的NLST队列（n=1,977）和LIDC-IDRI数据作为补充，在固定训练协议下，系统比较CNN和Vision Transformer在不同输入维度（2D、2.5D、3D）上的表现，重点关注区分度、稳定性、计算效率与失败模式。

Result: 2.5D CNN在区分度-稳定性权衡中表现最优（ROC-AUC 0.682, 95% CI [0.546, 0.799]），运行点稳定；3D CNN存在阈值不稳定性；Transformer出现退化预测（如全阳性预测）。所有方法的置信区间宽且重叠。

Conclusion: 对于类不平衡的肺癌筛查分类任务，2D和2.5D输入比全3D表示提供更可靠的性能、稳定性与计算效率之间的权衡。该结果应被视为受控条件下的资源-性能边界和失败模式分类，而非确定性优越性声明。

Abstract: Three-dimensional models are widely assumed preferable for volumetric medical imaging, yet their practical value depends on whether performance gains justify added computational cost and complexity. Rather than proposing a new architecture, we study how input dimensionality (2D, 2.5D, 3D) affects model behavior across convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) under a fixed training protocol. Using a leakage-free NLST cohort (n = 1,977) with supporting LIDC-IDRI data, we find that the 2.5D CNN offers the most favorable discrimination-stability trade-off in our comparison (ROC-AUC 0.682, 95% CI [0.546, 0.799]) with a stable operating point. In contrast, 3D CNNs show threshold instability, and transformers exhibit degenerate predictions, such as all-positive predictions. Confidence intervals are wide and overlapping, so we present these results as a controlled resource-performance frontier and a failure-mode taxonomy rather than as definitive superiority claims. For class-imbalanced lung cancer screening classification, 2D and 2.5D inputs provide a more reliable trade-off between performance, stability, and computational efficiency than full 3D representations.

</details>


### [44] [MVSegNet: A Lightweight Boundary-Aware Network for Fetal Lateral Ventricle Segmentation and Atrial Width Estimation in Prenatal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06958)
*Arafat Hossain Sayem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVSegNet，一种轻量级编码器-解码器网络，结合多尺度特征提取和边界感知细化，用于胎儿脑室超声图像中侧脑室心房宽度的自动分割。在584张专家标注的经脑室超声图像上训练和评估，MVSegNet在Dice得分（80.79%）、IoU（68.47%）、Hausdorff距离（4.07 mm）和心房宽度平均绝对误差（3.40 mm）方面表现优异，仅含231万参数，在NVIDIA T4 GPU上达到165.6 FPS，在边界和测量指标上优于六种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿脑室增大需通过产前超声测量侧脑室心房宽度来评估，但声影、散斑噪声和低对比度使得准确分割极为困难，现有方法难以兼顾精度与计算效率。

Method: 提出MVSegNet，一种轻量级编码器-解码器网络，核心创新在于结合多尺度特征提取（捕获不同大小的解剖结构）和边界感知细化（提升分割边界准确性），在584张专家标注图像上以70/15/15比例划分训练/验证/测试集。

Result: MVSegNet取得Dice 80.79%、IoU 68.47%、Hausdorff距离4.07 mm、心房宽度MAE 3.40 mm；模型仅2.31M参数，推理速度165.6 FPS；在边界和测量指标上全面优于六种分割基线方法。

Conclusion: MVSegNet在保持低计算成本的同时，在边界精度和测量准确性上超越现有基线，适用于自动化胎儿超声分析，具有临床部署潜力。

Abstract: Fetal ventriculomegaly is assessed by measuring the atrial width of the lateral ventricle in prenatal ultrasound. Accurate segmentation is essential for this measurement, but acoustic shadowing, speckle noise, and poor contrast make it difficult. We developed MVSegNet, a lightweight encoder-decoder network combining multi-scale feature extraction and boundary-aware refinement. The model was trained and evaluated on 584 expert-annotated transventricular ultrasound frames using a 70/15/15 split. Performance was compared against six segmentation baselines using overlap, boundary, and measurement metrics. MVSegNet achieved a Dice score of 80.79%, IoU of 68.47%, Hausdorff distance of 4.07 mm, and atrial width mean absolute error of 3.40 mm. The model contains 2.31 million parameters and runs at 165.6 frames per second on an NVIDIA T4 GPU. MVSegNet outperformed all evaluated baselines on boundary and measurement metrics while maintaining low computational cost, supporting its use in automated fetal ultrasound analysis.

</details>


### [45] [From Vision to Text: A Compact Multimodal Approach for Robust, Cross-Domain Presentation Attack Detection on ID Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06966)
*Qingwen Zeng,Juan E. Tapia,Sneha Das,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑的多模态模型（结合视觉与文本数据），用于身份证件的演示攻击检测（PAD）。研究发现多模态模型在监督微调后泛化能力强，但在零样本设置中失效。作者强调模型容量和真实世界数据对可靠PAD至关重要，现有合成数据集无法反映真实挑战，呼吁重新评估合成数据基准并构建更真实多样化的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私限制，身份证件领域可用于演示攻击检测（PAD）的数据十分有限，且跨域迁移问题严重。现有合成数据集可能无法反映真实世界的挑战，导致模型在零样本场景下泛化失败。因此，亟需探索更高效的多模态PAD方法，并重新审视合成数据的有效性。

Method: 提出了一种紧凑的多模态模型，包含新的生成模块和判别模块，融合视觉信息（图像特征）与文本信息（如证件上的文字内容）进行PAD检测。训练数据涵盖真实身份证图像与合成身份证图像，并分别在监督微调和零样本设置下评估模型性能。

Result: 多模态模型在监督微调后表现出较强的泛化能力，但在零样本（未见过的域/攻击类型）设置下性能显著下降。这表明模型容量和真实数据的参与是可靠PAD的关键，而现有合成数据集在反映真实世界挑战方面存在不足。

Conclusion: 当前合成数据集作为基准可能存在偏差，无法充分模拟真实世界中的攻击多样性。未来PAD研究需要构建更真实、更多样化的数据集，并重视模型容量在跨域泛化中的作用。应重新评估合成数据在PAD领域的定位与价值。

Abstract: Cross-domain shifts challenge Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) on ID Cards, given the restricted data available due to privacy concerns. This work proposes a compact multimodal model, based on new generative and discriminative blocks, which combines visual and textual data for PAD on genuine and synthetic ID images. While multimodal models exhibit strong generalisation after supervised fine-tuning, they fail in zero-shot settings. Our findings underscore that model capacity and real-world data are essential for reliable PAD, while existing synthetic datasets may not reflect real-world challenges. We argue for a re-evaluation of synthetic data as a benchmark and emphasise the need for more realistic, diverse datasets to advance PAD research.

</details>


### [46] [CL-CLIP: CLIP-Based Continual Learning Framework with Cost-Volume Category Decoupling for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06978)
*Zihan Liu,Yuguang Yang,Shengjie Su,Jianing Pang,Linlin Yang,Chunyu Xie,Nikolai Yu. Zolotykh,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CL-CLIP 是一种基于 CLIP 的持续目标检测框架，通过成本体积引导的类别解耦机制，使开放词汇检测器在持续微调新类别时能够缓解灾难性遗忘，平衡新旧类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇检测器（如 F-ViT）在零样本条件下对新类别有较好泛化能力，但一旦在新增类别上持续更新，会遭受严重的灾难性遗忘，丢失先前校准的检测能力。因此需要赋予其更好的持续学习能力。

Method: 提出 CL-CLIP 框架，采用 CAT-Seg 中计算 CLIP 图像-文本相似度成本体积的方法，生成密集的类别响应图作为零样本空间先验，将共享区域特征解耦为类别特定通路，并由多专家 RoI 头处理。

Result: 在 PASCAL VOC 和 MS-COCO 上，CL-CLIP 显著改进了 F-ViT 基线在持续微调下的性能，与现有持续目标检测器竞争，尤其在适应新类别的同时保持有竞争力的基类性能。

Conclusion: 通过成本体积引导的类别解耦，CL-CLIP 使开放词汇检测器获得更好的持续学习能力，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，实现了新类适应与旧类保持的平衡。

Abstract: Continual Object Detection (COD) requires a detector to acquire new categories over time while preserving previously learned ones. This goal is closely related to open-vocabulary detection, since both settings require reasoning over categories that are not fully covered by the annotations available at the current training stage. Recent CLIP-based open-vocabulary detectors have shown strong zero-shot generalization, and frameworks such as F-ViT demonstrate that vision-language pretraining can provide powerful zero-shot detection ability for unseen categories. However, real-world deployments cannot remain purely zero-shot: once these detectors are continually updated on newly introduced categories, they suffer severe catastrophic forgetting and quickly lose their previously calibrated detection ability. We therefore propose CL-CLIP, a CLIP-based COD framework that equips open-vocabulary detectors with better continual learning ability through cost-volume-guided category decoupling. Specifically, following CAT-Seg, we compute a CLIP image-text similarity cost volume, defined as dense category-wise response maps between visual tokens and class text embeddings. This zero-shot spatial prior decomposes shared region features into class-specific pathways, which are then processed by a Multi-Expert RoI head. Extensive experiments on PASCAL VOC and MS-COCO show that CL-CLIP substantially improves the F-ViT baseline under continual fine-tuning and achieves competitive performance with existing continual object detectors, especially in adapting to newly introduced categories while preserving competitive base-class performance.

</details>


### [47] [Don't Pause: Streaming Video-Language Synchrony for Online Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06991)
*Zhenyu Yang,Kairui Zhang,Shengsheng Qian,Weiming Dong,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出流式视频-语言同步（SVLS）范式与 LyraV 实时助手，通过无训练验证状态机（FDTC）和轻量级令牌节奏器（SToP）实现帧级增量解码，在不停顿感知的前提下达到 98.29% 的同步率和 3.89 FPS 的实时处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Video-LLM 在流式场景中生成回复时会暂停视频感知，破坏实时视频-语言同步并导致卡顿，亟需一种能在持续感知视频的同时同步生成语言的方法。

Method: 构建层级控制框架：① FDTC（基于验证的有限状态机）在无需训练的条件下决定继续说话、开始新回复或保持沉默；② SToP（轻量级预测模块）动态调节语言生成速率以匹配视觉内容节奏；③ 逐帧增量子预算解码，每个帧间隔仅发射少量令牌，确保感知不被整句阻塞。

Result: 在五个在线和三个离线基准上保持骨干模型通用理解能力，流式同步率达 98.29%，实时处理速度达 3.89 FPS，并展现出对流式令牌的动态推理能力。

Conclusion: LyraV 通过帧级细粒度同步机制有效解决了流式场景下的感知-生成冲突，为实时视频-语言交互提供了可行方案，且具备连续“思考”能力。

Abstract: Online Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have advanced toward seamless human-AI interaction through frame-by-frame processing and proactive responding. However, a critical challenge remains in streaming scenarios: existing models typically pause video perception while generating responses, breaking real-time video-language synchrony and causing stutters. To address this, we introduce a novel paradigm for online video understanding: Streaming Video-Language Synchrony (SVLS), and present LyraV, a live streaming assistant built upon a hierarchical control framework with two core innovations. First, the Frame-Driven Transition Controller (FDTC), a training-free verification-based finite-state machine, makes high-level semantic decisions on when to continue speaking, start a new response, or stay silent. Second, the Streaming Token Pacer (SToP), a plug-and-play lightweight predictive module, dynamically adapts the language generation rate to match the pace of the visual content. Concretely, LyraV performs \emph{per-frame incremental, sub-budget decoding}: within each frame interval it emits only a small chunk of tokens that fits the real-time budget, so perception is never blocked for a full sentence. Together, these components enable LyraV to seamlessly interleave incoming video frames with generated word tokens, achieving a fine-grained synchrony. Extensive experiments conducted on five online and three offline benchmarks demonstrate that LyraV preserves the backbone's general understanding ability while substantially improving streaming synchrony and narrative fluency, delivering a 98.29\% synchrony with video playback and a real-time processing speed of 3.89 FPS. Interestingly, we observe an empirical capability in LyraV: dynamic reasoning over streaming tokens, enabling continuous interpretation and "thinking" alongside visual input.

</details>


### [48] [GuideCAD: A Lightweight Multimodal Framework for 3D CAD Model Generation via Prefix Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07024)
*Minseong Kim,Jinyeong Park,Sungho Park,Jibum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GuideCAD方法，利用视觉-文本表示以少量可训练参数高效生成3D CAD模型，相比微调方法参数减少约4倍、训练效率提升2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态3D CAD生成方法计算资源消耗大，需要高效的训练方案。

Method: 使用映射网络将图像嵌入转换为前缀嵌入，使预训练大语言模型整合视觉与文本信息，再由基于Transformer的解码器预测构建序列以生成3D CAD模型。

Result: 在自构建的GuideCAD数据集上，该方法参数减少约4倍、训练效率提升2倍，同时生成质量与微调方法相当。

Conclusion: GuideCAD通过少量可训练参数实现了高效的3D CAD生成，开源了代码和数据集。

Abstract: Multi-modal approaches used for 3D CAD generation require substantial computational resources, necessitating efficient training. To address this, we propose GuideCAD, which leverages semantically rich visual-textual representations having only a small number of trainable parameters to generate 3D CAD models. Specifically, GuideCAD uses a mapping network that converts image embeddings into prefix embeddings, enabling a pretrained large language model to integrate visual and textual information. As a result, a transformer-based decoder predicts the construction sequence using the visual-textual embeddings in order to generate the 3D CAD model. For experimental evaluation, we construct a new dataset, referred to as GuideCAD, which consists of text-image pairs. Each pair includes a text prompt that represents a 3D CAD construction sequence and its corresponding 3D CAD image. Our experimental results show that GuideCAD generates comparably high-quality 3D CAD models while using approximately four times fewer parameters and achieving twice the training efficiency compared to fine-tuning approaches. We have released the source code and dataset for our method at: https://github.com/mskimS2/GuideCAD

</details>


### [49] [Never Seen Before: Benchmarking Genuine Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval with Consistent Video-Sourced Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07032)
*Zhenyu Yang,Zemin Du,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ZeroSight基准数据集和SC4CIR方法，解决零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）中参考图像与目标图像不相关、数据集被CLIP预训练数据污染的问题，并揭示现有方法性能被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZS-CIR数据集存在两个关键问题：(1) 参考图像与目标图像因噪声数据源而完全不相关；(2) 使用CLIP已训练过的公开图像数据集，并非真正的零样本场景。导致现有评估无法真实反映CIR方法的能力。

Method: (1) 构建ZeroSight基准：从2022年3月31日后发布的视频中提取帧构建参考-目标对，确保数据未被CLIP预训练，并通过LLM生成相对描述，实现视觉与语义一致性；(2) 提出SC4CIR方法：一种无需训练的MLLM驱动方法，通过3种对称一致性检查有效识别困难负样本，可即插即用地集成到各类CIR方法中。

Result: 基于27种方法的实验表明，现有ZS-CIR数据集和评估指标导致检索性能被夸大，无法真实反映CIR方法的能力。SC4CIR方法能有效提升检索性能。

Conclusion: ZeroSight为ZS-CIR提供了更可靠、更真实的评估基准，揭示了现有方法性能被高估的问题；SC4CIR作为一种训练-free的即插即用方法，能显著提升CIR方法的实际表现。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image based on a query composed of a reference image and a relative caption without training samples. Existing ZS-CIR datasets often suffer from complete irrelevance between reference and target images due to noisy image sources, and do not achieve a true zero-shot scenario as they use public image datasets that models like CLIP have been trained on. To tackle these challenges, we introduce ZeroSight, a novel benchmark for ZS-CIR. It includes a dataset with consistent reference-target pairs sourced from videos, a data construction pipeline, and evaluation methods that consider the ranking of multiple positive and negative target images. We ensure visually and semantically consistent reference-target pairs by extracting frames from a single video and generating relative captions using LLM-assisted methods. To ensure a true zero-shot scenario, we use video data published after March 31, 2022, ensuring it was not included in CLIP's pre-training data. Additionally, we propose a training-free MLLM-driven method, SC4CIR (Symmetric Consistency for CIR), which can effectively identify hard negative targets through 3 symmetric consistency checks. This method is plug-and-play, seamlessly integrating with various CIR methods and significantly improving performance. Our experimental results from 27 methods reveal that current ZS-CIR datasets and evaluation metrics result in inflated retrieval performance, exaggerating the capabilities of CIR methods. Our benchmark and models can be accessed at https://github.com/sotayang/ZeroSight.

</details>


### [50] [ForensicConcept: Transferable Forensic Concepts for AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07034)
*Menyanshu Zhou,Ziyin Zhou,Ke Sun,Yunpeng Luo,Jiayi Ji,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ForensicConcept框架，通过提取检测器中的显式取证概念并跨主干网络迁移，解决AI生成图像检测器在未见过的生成器上泛化失败的问题，同时提供可审计的证据输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在分布内数据上准确率高，但对未见过的生成器泛化失败，关键障碍在于检测器是黑盒模型，无法揭示决策依据。

Method: 通过Transformer注意力定位决策关键补丁，聚类生成紧凑概念码本，使用概念对齐投影产生可审计证据；引入基于CleanDIFT扩散特征的生成痕迹参考，通过邻域结构一致性（CKNNA）量化主干痕迹对齐度，并提出概念码本注入将扩散导出的概念迁移到目标主干。

Result: 在GenImage、GAN-family和Chameleon基准测试上，该方法持续优于先前方法，且CKNNA对齐度能预测迁移效果。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提升了AI生成图像检测的跨生成器泛化能力，还通过CKNNA对齐度解释了不同主干网络迁移取证证据有效性的差异原因。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors achieve high accuracy on in-distribution data but often fail on unseen generators. A key obstacle to understanding this failure is the black-box nature of current detectors: they do not reveal which evidence drives their decisions. We propose ForensicConcept, a framework that extracts explicit forensic concepts from detectors and enables their transfer across backbones. Our method localizes decision-critical patches via Transformer attribution, clusters them into a compact concept codebook, and uses a concept-aligned projection to produce auditable evidence readouts. Motivated by prior studies showing that DINO representations can guide diffusion generation and exhibit concept-level correspondence with diffusion features, we introduce a generation-trace reference based on CleanDIFT diffusion features and quantify backbone-trace alignment via neighborhood-structure consistency (CKNNA). We further propose concept codebook injection to transfer diffusion-derived concepts into target backbones. Experiments on GenImage, GAN-family, and Chameleon benchmarks show consistent improvements over prior methods. We also find that CKNNA alignment predicts transfer effectiveness, providing a principled explanation for why some backbones yield more transferable forensic evidence than others.

</details>


### [51] [STREAM: Stochastic Riemannian Flow Matching with Anisotropic Decoder for Digital Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07036)
*Won June Cho,Daeky Jeong,Hyeongyeol Lim,Hongjun Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STREAM框架，利用预训练的病理视觉基础模型作为潜在空间，结合黎曼流匹配在超球面上生成高质量的组织病理学图像，解决了条件崩溃问题并实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用预训练视觉基础模型作为条件信号会导致'条件崩溃'，即条件信号主导潜在空间，降低生成样本的质量和多样性。因此需要一种新的生成范式来避免这一问题。

Method: 1）利用预训练的病理视觉基础模型的patch-token特征作为潜在空间（而非条件信号），这些特征位于单位超球面上；2）提出STREAM框架，包含两阶段：桥式随机扰动实现每个token在超球面上的可整流性以训练DiT；各向异性解码器分配鲁棒性到速度场Jacobian的低能方向同时保持高能方向的保真度。

Result: 在乳腺癌和结直肠癌数据集上实现了SOTA的重建和生成性能。

Conclusion: STREAM通过将病理VFM特征作为潜在空间并采用黎曼流匹配，有效避免了条件崩溃，为病理图像生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation addresses critical challenges in computational pathology, including patient privacy and the growing need for large-scale training data for foundation models. Latent diffusion models have dominated the image generation domain, with recent works emphasizing that the choice of latent space is critical to the quality of generated images. Existing state-of-the-art generative models in histopathology use pretrained Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) as conditioning signals, and we observe that this leads to "conditioning collapse," where the conditioning signal dominates the latent space and lowers the quality and diversity of generated samples. Therefore, we instead use pretrained histopathology VFMs as the latent space itself, leveraging their patch-token features that encode rich semantic information. We empirically show that these features are $\ell_2$-normalized and lie on the unit hypersphere $\mathcal{S}^{d-1}$ with strong angular dominance and intrinsic curvature, making them naturally suited for a Riemannian formulation. We therefore present STREAM, the first framework to apply Riemannian flow matching in the pathology domain. STREAM consists of two stages: 1) a bridge-type stochastic perturbation that establishes per-token rectifiability on $\mathcal{S}^{d-1}$ for training a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) in latent space, and 2) a novel anisotropic decoder that allocates robustness to low-energy directions of the velocity-field Jacobian while preserving fidelity along its high-energy directions. Together, STREAM achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction and generation performance on breast and colorectal cancer datasets. The code will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [52] [TrioPose: Native Triple-Stream Diffusion Transformers for Pose-Guided Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07053)
*Dian Gu,Zhengyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrioPose是一个基于SD3.5M架构的原生姿态驱动框架，通过三流姿态感知DiT、可学习关系偏置掩码和姿态引导空间损失加权策略，解决了多人物场景下的肢体畸变和特征串扰问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于UNet的适配器在处理复杂多人场景时存在长程空间依赖不足的问题，而新兴MM-DiT中简单的信号拼接会严重破坏预训练潜变量分布，导致肢体畸变和特征串扰。

Method: 提出三流姿态感知DiT（TSPA-DiT），将姿态作为独立模态并通过逐层激活与零初始化双残差注入保持潜变量稳定性；设计可学习关系偏置掩码，将拓扑连接分类为细粒度物理状态并映射为连续注意力软约束以解耦实例间干扰；采用姿态引导空间损失加权策略，利用热力图导出的误差图聚焦解剖学监督于畸变敏感区域。

Result: 在Human-Art、CrowdPose和OCHuman挑战性基准上达到SOTA，其中在Human-Art上AP达64.33，较先前最优方法提升30%。

Conclusion: TrioPose在复杂多人图像生成中实现了视觉保真度和文本-图像语义对齐的新标准，有效解决了肢体畸变和实例间干扰问题。

Abstract: Pose-guided text-to-image generation often suffers from limb distortions and feature crosstalk in complex multi-person scenarios. While existing UNet-based adapters struggle with long-range spatial dependencies, emerging Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) offer superior global modeling. However, naive signal concatenation in MM-DiTs severely disrupts pre-trained latent distributions. To address this, we propose TrioPose, a native pose-driven framework built upon the SD3.5M architecture. Specifically, we introduce a Triple-Stream Pose-Aware DiT (TSPA-DiT) that treats pose as an independent modality. It employs layer-wise activation and zero-initialized dual-residual injection to smoothly enforce geometric constraints while preserving pre-trained latent stability. To resolve severe multi-instance occlusions, we design a Learnable Relational Bias Mask that categorizes topological connectivity into fine-grained physical states, mapping them into continuous attention soft constraints to effectively decouple inter-instance interference. Furthermore, a Pose-Guided Spatial Loss Weighting strategy modulates the native diffusion objective using heatmap-derived error maps, focusing anatomical supervision strictly on distortion-prone regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TrioPose achieves state-of-the-art performance across challenging benchmarks, including Human-Art, CrowdPose, and OCHuman. Notably, it attains an AP of $64.33$ on Human-Art, representing a $30\%$ improvement over prior arts, while setting new standards for visual fidelity and text-image semantic alignment in complex multi-human generation.

</details>


### [53] [AsyncPatch Diffusion: spatially-flexible image generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07079)
*Samuele Papa,Valentin De Bortoli,Guillaume Couairon,Daniel Sýkora,Romuald Elie,Klaus Greff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出异步补丁扩散（AsyncPatch Diffusion），为不同空间区域分配不同噪声水平，实现异质去噪轨迹，在保持生成质量的同时原生支持图像修复和自适应生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型对所有空间区域使用相同噪声水平，限制了去噪轨迹的多样性。本文旨在打破这一限制，允许不同区域遵循不同的去噪时间表。

Method: 1）异步联合扩散框架，为不同输入维度（像素或潜在token）分配不同噪声水平；2）有控噪声水平采样器（controlled noise-level sampler），调节平均腐蚀水平和空间变异性；3）输入引导机制（input guidance），利用干净或部分腐蚀区域引导未知区域生成。

Result: 在ImageNet 256和LSUN上与常规扩散模型生成质量相当，无需特定微调即可原生支持图像修复，并实现不确定性引导加速和自回归采样等自适应生成策略。

Conclusion: 异步噪声分配定义了一个有效的生成过程，其ELBO首次得到证明；该方法为空间自适应扩散生成提供了统一框架，兼具灵活性与实用性。

Abstract: Standard diffusion models corrupt an entire sample with a single shared noise level, forcing all spatial regions to follow the same denoising trajectory. We introduce AsyncPatch Diffusion, a joint-diffusion framework that assigns distinct noise levels to different input dimensions, such as image pixels, or latent tokens. We show how this asynchronous corruption defines a valid generative process while supporting a richer family of spatially heterogeneous denoising trajectories, and prove the first valid ELBO for this process. We show that a single pretrained model can perform spatially adaptive generation, where different regions are denoised on different schedules. A key challenge is training: naive independent noise-level sampling overemphasizes highly heterogeneous configurations and underrepresents homogeneous noise levels, that are crucial during sampling. We address this with a controlled noise-level sampler that regulates both the average corruption level and its spatial variability. AsyncPatch achieves generation quality comparable to conventional diffusion on ImageNet 256 and LSUN, while being natively suited for inpainting without task-specific fine-tuning. We further introduce input guidance, which uses clean or partially corrupted regions to guide the generation of unknown regions, improving local consistency and texture matching. Finally, we demonstrate adaptive generation strategies including uncertainty-guided acceleration and autoregressive sampling.

</details>


### [54] [An Adaptive Data cleaning Framework for Noisy Label Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07086)
*Chen-Hsuan Fang,Wei-Hsinag Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Jung-Hua Wang,Tsung-Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个自适应的数据清洗框架，通过整合局部、全局和学习动力学线索，在统一低维特征空间中进行多度量聚类，无需人工阈值或噪声先验即可自动划分干净/噪声样本，在多个数据集和噪声率下实现了高召回率和精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据清洗和样本选择方法依赖人工设定阈值、噪声比例先验知识或单一度量（学习动态或几何结构），在复杂数据场景下不稳定。需要一种自适应、无需阈值和先验知识的噪声标签检测方法。

Method: 1) 将样本映射到统一的低维特征空间（模块化特征拼接）；2) 提出两种实例：2D度量（类自适应KNN局部不一致性 + K均值全局质心距离）和3D多度量（额外加入z归一化分数）；3) 在特征空间上执行多度量聚类，自动划分干净主导和噪声主导成分，无需人工阈值或噪声先验。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST和ImageNet-100上（5%至40%对称标签噪声）实现了高召回率，ImageNet-100在40%噪声下召回率>=98%；后续训练在多种设置下均获得准确率提升，尤其在ImageNet-100高噪声条件下。

Conclusion: 多度量集成提供了一种无阈值、实用、低调参的噪声标签检测策略，有效克服了传统单度量方法的局限性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) excel in computer vision tasks given large annotated datasets. In real-world applications, however, labels are often corrupted by ambiguity, human error, or dynamic environments. Over-parameterized DNNs easily memorize these noisy labels during training, degrading model accuracy and generalization. Existing data-cleaning and sample-selection strategies often rely on manually specified thresholds, prior knowledge of the noise ratio, or a single metric (either learning dynamics or geometric structure), making them unstable in complex data regimes. This paper proposes a self-adaptive data-cleaning framework that integrates local, global, and learning dynamics cues for robust noisy-label detection. Samples are mapped into a unified low-dimensional feature space through a modular feature concatenation paradigm. We provide two instantiations: a 2D metric integrating class-adaptive KNN-based local disagreement with k-means-based global centroid distance, and a 3D multi-metric that additionally incorporates a z-normalized score. Unlike conventional 1D Gaussian Mixture Models applied to a single scalar metric, our framework performs multi-metric clustering on the feature space to adaptively partition samples into clean-dominant and noise-dominant components without requiring manual thresholds or noise priors. Experiments on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and ImageNet-100 with 5% to 40% symmetric label noise show high recall across settings, including near-perfect recall (>=98%) on ImageNet-100 at 40% noise. Subsequent training yields accuracy gains across evaluated settings, especially under severe corruption on ImageNet-100. These findings suggest that multi-metric integration provides a threshold-free, practical, and low-tuning strategy for noisy label detection.

</details>


### [55] [Detecting Temporally Localized Manipulations in Authentic Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07090)
*Okan Umur,Ali Emre Güşlü,Ibrahim Delibasoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究指出现有视频深伪检测数据集未能充分建模“真实视频中插入短片段篡改”的场景，为此构建了专门的数据集，并采用DINOv3线性探针与基于连续帧相似性的方法建立了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频伪造检测数据集主要关注整体伪造或特定操作（如对象移除、视频修复），缺乏对“在真实视频中插入一段短暂且高度逼真的篡改片段”这一现实场景的建模，导致相关检测方法的研究受限。

Method: 首先系统回顾并分析了代表性数据集的特征与局限；然后构建了一个包含真实视频与短篡改片段的自定义测试集；最后评估了两类方法：1）基于DINOv3特征的线性探针（采用三种阈值策略）；2）基于连续帧相似性的篡改边界检测方法。

Result: 实验建立了该挑战性场景的初始基准，揭示了内容自适应阈值机制的必要性；数据集、代码与补充材料已开源。

Conclusion: 现有数据集不足以支撑局部时序篡改检测任务，新数据集与基准实验表明，针对部分篡改视频的检测需要更精细的阈值自适应策略，该工作为后续研究提供了基础平台与参考基线。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of video editing and generative artificial intelligence technologies has made realistic video manipulation increasingly accessible. Although existing datasets have significantly advanced research in deepfake detection, object removal, and video inpainting, they do not adequately model scenarios in which a short manipulated segment is inserted into an otherwise authentic video and the original video continues afterward. In this study, we review representative datasets from the literature, analyze their characteristics, and discuss their limitations with respect to temporally localized realistic manipulation detection. Based on this analysis, we motivate the need for a new dataset specifically designed for authentic videos containing short and highly realistic manipulated intervals. Finally, we evaluate two complementary approaches on our custom-curated test set to establish an initial benchmark for this challenging scenario. The first employs a linear probe on DINOv3 features, assessed under three thresholding strategies. The second leverages DINOv3 features with a consecutive frame similarity-based method to detect temporal manipulation boundaries. Together, these experiments provide an initial benchmark for partially manipulated video detection and highlight the need for content-adaptive thresholding mechanisms. The dataset, code, and supplementary materials are publicly available at https://github.com/OkanUmur/temporally-localized-video-manipulation-detection.

</details>


### [56] [LARA: Latent Action Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07100)
*Mengya Liu,Baoxiong Jia,Jiangyong Huang,Jingze Zhang,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LARA是一种即插即用框架，通过对齐潜在动作模型（LAM）与视觉-语言动作模型（VLA）的表示，实现联合优化，使LAM利用动作轨迹避免错误视觉变化，VLA借助LAM的前向动力学正则化减少功能无效轨迹的幻觉，在3个仿真和1个真实机器人操作基准上平均提升约10%、5%和15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖大规模高质量数据，但真实机器人动作数据集稀缺；而LAM虽能从无标注人类视频学习潜在动作表示辅助VLA，但两者独立训练导致LAM与VLA训练脱节，且VLA受限于冻结的LAM表示。

Method: 提出LARA框架，通过表示对齐联合优化LAM和VLA，使LAM利用动作轨迹学习避免虚假视觉变化，同时VLA借助LAM的前向动力学正则化减少无效轨迹的幻觉。

Result: 在3个仿真和1个真实机器人操作基准上，LARA在预训练、后训练增强和LAM精调场景下分别平均提升约10%、5%和15%。

Conclusion: LARA作为即插即用框架，有效对齐LAM与VLA表示，实现双向互惠优化，显著提升机器人操作性能，并兼容多种训练场景。

Abstract: Visual-language action (VLA) models enable robots to predict actions directly from observations and language instructions, but their performance depends on large-scale, high-quality data and is limited by the scarcity of real-world robot action datasets. To facilitate VLA model learning with abundant unlabeled human videos, Latent Action Models (LAM) learn latent action representations from visual dynamics to provide additional supervision for VLA learning. However, LAM and VLA are typically trained separately, leaving LAM ungrounded during VLA training and VLA models constrained by frozen LAM representations. To address these issues, we propose Latent Action Representation Alignment (LARA), a plug-and-play framework that jointly optimizes LAM and VLA via representation alignment. This enables reciprocal benefits where LAMs learn with action trajectories to avoid spurious visual changes, while VLAs are regularized by forward dynamics learned within LAMs to reduce hallucinations of functionally ineffective trajectories. We demonstrate LARA versatility and effectiveness for pre-training, post-training enhancement of pre-trained VLA models, and LAM refinement, achieving an average of ~10%, ~5%, and ~15% improvement over 3 simulation and 1 meticulously designed real-world robotic manipulation benchmarks.

</details>


### [57] [GP-Adapter: Gaussian Process CLIP-Adapter for Few-Shot Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07102)
*Taisei Saito,Koretaka Ogata,Takafumi Hiroi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GP-Adapter 是一个无需训练、基于高斯过程（GP）不确定性建模的框架，通过为冻结的 CLIP 嵌入构建类别级单类 GP（图像用 RBF 核，文本用线性核），融合预测统计量产生方差感知的置信度分数，在 few-shot 分类和 OOD 检测中提升 CLIP 的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 在零样本识别中表现优异，但其确定性相似度分数缺乏不确定性信息，在分布偏移和数据稀缺场景下可靠性不足。

Method: 在冻结的 CLIP 嵌入之上，为图像特征构建基于 RBF 核的类别级单类 GP，为文本提示构建线性核 GP，融合两者的预测统计量（均值与方差）得到方差感知的置信度分数用于 OOD 检测；仅依赖 K-shot 缓存和轻量超参数选择，无需微调 CLIP 骨干。

Result: 在 ImageNet 和多个 OOD 基准上，GP-Adapter 在 few-shot 分类中具有竞争力，且与提示学习基线结合时能持续提升 OOD 检测性能。

Conclusion: 将概率推理（GP 不确定性建模）与大规模预训练视觉-语言模型（CLIP）集成，可以在低数据和分布偏移场景下提升模型的可靠性。

Abstract: We propose GP-Adapter, a training-free framework that augments CLIP (Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training) with Gaussian Process (GP) uncertainty modeling for few-shot classification and out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. While CLIP achieves strong zero-shot recognition, it yields deterministic similarity scores and offers limited uncertainty information, which is critical under distribution shift and data scarcity. GP-Adapter constructs modality-specific, class-wise one-class GPs on top of frozen CLIP embeddings using an RBF kernel for image features and a linear kernel for text prompts and fuses their predictive statistics to produce a variance-aware confidence score for OOD detection. The method requires no fine-tuning of the CLIP backbone and relies only on a small $K$-shot cache and lightweight hyperparameter selection, with memory cost scaling as $O(CK^2)$ for $C$ classes and $K$ shots. Experiments on ImageNet and multiple OOD benchmarks show that GP-Adapter provides competitive few-shot performance and consistently improves OOD detection when combined with prompt-learning baselines, highlighting the complementarity between GP-based uncertainty modeling and prompt learning. Overall, our results suggest that integrating probabilistic inference with large pre-trained vision-language models can improve reliability in low-data and distribution-shifted settings. Code is available at https://github.com/tms-byte/GP-Adapter

</details>


### [58] [3DMorph: Single-Image-Guided Local 3D Shape Editing and Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07115)
*Tobias Preintner,Yunfei Deng,Phillip Müller,Sebastian Illing,Adrian König,Thomas Bäck,Elena Raponi,Niki van Stein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DMorph是一种无需训练的单图引导局部3D形状编辑与变形框架，能将直观的2D编辑迁移至3D，并支持中间形状生成，在Delta3D基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法多为全局性、领域特定、需要复杂的用户交互，或仅关注外观而非几何形状，缺乏简单有效的局部3D形状编辑方法。

Method: 提出3DMorph，一种无需训练的单图引导局部3D形状编辑与变形框架：通过给定展示所需形状修改的编辑图像，自动定位相关3D区域并将2D修改迁移至3D，同时保留未修改区域；还支持在原始与编辑对象之间生成中间形状。同时提出Delta3D基准用于评估编辑质量。

Result: 实验结果表明3DMorph能够将直观的2D编辑有效转化为3D编辑，在图像引导的局部3D编辑任务上超越了当前最先进的生成与编辑方法。

Conclusion: 3DMorph提供了一种简单有效的局部3D形状编辑方案，填补了3D形状局部编辑的空白，为设计探索提供了灵活的工具。


Abstract: Despite recent progress in 3D generation, intuitive editing of existing shapes remains limited. Unlike images, which benefit from well-established inpainting tools, general 3D objects such as meshes still lack simple and effective methods for local shape editing. Existing approaches are often global, domain-specific, require complex user interaction, or focus on appearance (color and texture) rather than geometry. We introduce 3DMorph, a training-free framework for single-image-guided local 3D shape editing and morphing. Given an edited image showing a desired shape modification, our method automatically localizes the relevant 3D region and transfers 2D modifications to 3D while preserving unmodified areas. 3DMorph also enables intermediate shape generation between the original and edited objects, facilitating design exploration. To benchmark editing quality, we introduce Delta3D, an image-guided local 3D editing benchmark with paired ground-truth edits. Experimental results show that 3DMorph translates intuitive 2D edits into 3D, outperforming state-of-the-art generative and editing methods.

</details>


### [59] [Native3D: End-to-End 3D Scene Generation via Unified Mesh-Texture Modeling and Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07117)
*Yibo Liu,Ziwei Zhang,Haozhou Pang,Menghao Li,Lanshan He,Gan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Native3D是首个完全绕过2D中间表示的直接3D场景生成框架，通过统一的网格-纹理联合表示和3D表示对齐损失，实现了更高质量的3D场景生成与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D场景生成方法通常需要将3D表示适配到2D领域以利用预训练的扩散模型，这不可避免地引入了域适应问题，包括几何结构失真和纹理细节退化。为了解决这些局限性，作者提出了一个不需要2D中间表示的端到端3D场景生成框架。

Method: 设计了一个统一的网格-纹理联合表示（mesh-texture joint representation），通过基于Transformer的场景编码器同时建模几何结构和纹理特征，保持场景内物体的空间关系和视觉一致性。进一步提出了3D表示对齐损失（3D REPA Loss），采用改进的对比学习机制在潜在空间中对齐多级语义表示，增强几何和纹理保真度。

Result: 实验结果表明Native3D在生成质量和编辑灵活性方面均优于现有方法，为3D场景编辑提供了一种新的解决方案。

Conclusion: Native3D作为首个完全绕过2D中间表示的端到端3D场景生成框架，通过统一的网格-纹理联合表示和3D表示对齐损失，有效解决了传统方法中的域适应问题，在生成质量和编辑灵活性上取得了显著提升。

Abstract: This paper presents Native3D, the first end-to-end 3D scene generation framework that completely bypasses 2D intermediate representations. Traditional approaches typically require adapting 3D representations to the 2D domain to leverage pre-trained diffusion models, which inevitably introduces domain adaptation issues including geometric structural distortion and texture detail degradation. To address these limitations, we design a unified mesh-texture joint representation that simultaneously models both geometric structures and texture features through a Transformer-based scene encoder, effectively maintaining spatial relationships and visual consistency among objects within scenes. We further propose the 3D Representation Alignment Loss (3D REPA Loss), which employs an improved contrastive learning mechanism to align multi-level semantic representations in the latent space, significantly enhancing geometric and textural fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate that Native3D outperforms existing methods in both generation quality and editing flexibility, providing a novel solution for 3D scene editing.

</details>


### [60] [Consistent-Inversion: Reverse Consistency Guidance for Structure-Preserving Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07145)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Jingcai Guo,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Consistent-Inversion，一种无需训练的逆向一致性引导框架，用于结构保持的视觉编辑。通过检查中间目标轨迹能否在源提示下逆向回到源反转轨迹，来修正早期去噪步骤，从而在保持背景/结构保真度的同时实现目标编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无需训练的文本引导图像编辑方法（如反转法）将源图像映射到噪声潜变量后复用，但源重建与目标编辑存在耦合，导致轨迹不匹配，可能破坏背景/布局细节或过度约束预期编辑效果。

Method: 提出 Consistent-Inversion 框架：构建辅助目标端噪声表示，执行源引导的逆向去噪，计算逆向一致性差异，将其作为修正信号应用于早期目标去噪步骤。该方法不更新模型参数，与基于反转的编辑器兼容，且稀疏应用时仅引入少量推理开销。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 基准上的实验表明，Consistent-Inversion 在统一的 SD3.5 协议下提升了背景和结构保真度，同时保持了目标提示对齐；兼容性实验验证了该修正原则在经典 Stable-Diffusion 反转流程中同样有效。

Conclusion: Consistent-Inversion 通过逆向一致性引导有效解耦了源重建与目标编辑，在不牺牲编辑效果的前提下显著改善了结构保持能力，且具有通用性和低开销优势。

Abstract: Text-guided diffusion models have become effective tools for real-image visual editing, where the edited image must follow a target instruction while preserving editing-irrelevant structure. Most training-free editors rely on inversion: a source image is mapped to a noisy latent trajectory and the terminal latent is reused for target-prompt denoising. This reuse is useful for preservation, but it also couples source reconstruction and target editing. The resulting trajectory mismatch may either damage background/layout details or over-constrain the intended edit. This paper presents Consistent-Inversion, a training-free reverse consistency guidance framework for structure-preserving visual editing. Instead of treating the inverted source latent as a fixed initialization, Consistent-Inversion checks whether an intermediate target trajectory can be reversed toward the source inversion trajectory under the source prompt. To make this check well-defined, we construct an auxiliary target-side noise representation, perform source-guided reverse denoising, and use the resulting reverse consistency discrepancy as a correction signal for selected early target denoising steps. The method does not update model parameters, is compatible with inversion-based editors, and introduces only a small inference overhead when applied sparsely. Experiments on PIE-Bench show that Consistent-Inversion improves background and structural fidelity under a unified SD3.5 protocol while maintaining target-prompt alignment, and compatibility experiments further verify the same correction principle on classical Stable-Diffusion inversion pipelines.

</details>


### [61] [TraRA: Trajectory-level Recognition Aggregation for Video Text Spotting in Urban Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07161)
*Duc Tri Tran,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Vijay John,Phi Le Nguyen,Yasutomo Kawanishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraRA是一个即插即用的视频文本识别方法，通过轨迹级时序与多模态一致性聚合，在动态监控场景下显著提升文本追踪与识别性能，在四个公开基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频文本识别方法逐帧独立处理，忽略了帧间时序关联，导致监控场景下（运动模糊、遮挡、尺度变化等）识别结果不一致且不准确。

Method: 提出TraRA方法，包含时序聚类模块（根据时间与视觉一致性优化噪声轨迹）和视觉-语言聚合模块（利用LoRA增强的视觉-语言模型跨帧融合视觉线索与语言上下文），在文本轨迹层面进行识别聚合。

Result: 在RoadText、BOVText、ArTVideo和ICDAR15四个涵盖道路与城市场景的公开基准上，TraRA持续提升了追踪与识别性能，优于最先进的视频文本识别方法。

Conclusion: TraRA通过轨迹级时序与多模态一致性聚合，有效解决了监控场景下视频文本识别的帧间不一致问题，是一种通用且高效的即插即用方案。

Abstract: Video Text Spotting (VTS) is essential for urban surveillance and intelligent transportation systems, enabling automated reading of street signs, vehicle markings, and scene text in video streams. However, reliable recognition remains challenging due to dynamic video factors common in surveillance scenarios, including motion blur, occlusion, and scale variation, which degrade frame-level recognition. Existing VTS methods typically perform recognition independently on each frame, leading to inconsistent and inaccurate results across sequences. To address these limitations, we propose TraRA (Trajectory-level Recognition Aggregation for VTS), a plug-and-play method that performs trajectory-level text recognition by leveraging temporal and multimodal consistency. TraRA integrates two key modules: (1) the Temporal Clustering and (2) the Vision-Language Aggregation. The former refines noisy trajectories by grouping temporally and visually coherent text instances, while the latter employs a Low-Rank Adaptation-enhanced Vision-Language model to fuse visual cues with linguistic context across frames. By aggregating information over entire text trajectories, TraRA achieves robust text recognition even under challenging surveillance conditions. Extensive experiments on four public benchmarks, including road and urban scene datasets (RoadText, BOVText, ArTVideo, and ICDAR15), demonstrate that TraRA consistently improves tracking and recognition performance over state-of-the-art VTS methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/trid2912/TraRA.

</details>


### [62] [When Recovery Matters: The Blind Spot of Surrogate Privacy in MLLM Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07171)
*Siyuan Xu,Yibing Liu,Peilin Chen,Yung-Hui LI,Shiqi Wang,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPPE基准，首个面向隐私保护图像编辑的可恢复性基准，涵盖36个细粒度隐私类别和65条编辑指令，并定义可编辑性评估和替代到源编辑恢复两个互补任务，分别提出ERMA和C2E-S2SER方法，在实验中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在指令驱动图像编辑中存在隐私风险，现有隐私保护策略用替代内容替换敏感区域后进行云端编辑，但输出结果是编辑后的替代图像而非用户希望编辑的源图像，忽视了局部恢复的设计与评估。

Method: 提出SPPE基准（含两个任务：可编辑性评估和替代到源编辑恢复），针对可编辑性评估提出ERMA（通过指令感知的多模态关系建模预测替代可编辑性），针对替代到源编辑恢复提出C2E-S2SER（利用替代编辑对作为视觉编辑证据，源图像作为源保留锚点进行循环一致性恢复）。

Result: 在SPPE和InstructPix2Pix上实验：ERMA在可编辑性评估上SRCC提升13.9%、PLCC提升12.3%；C2E-S2SER在替代到源编辑恢复任务上，在SPPE的8个源完整性和编辑一致性指标上全面优于SOER。

Conclusion: SPPE基准有效弥补了隐私保护图像编辑中可恢复性评估的空白，ERMA和C2E-S2SER方法分别在两个任务上取得显著提升，为隐私保护图像编辑提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) enable flexible instruction-driven image editing, but privacy risks arise when user images expose diverse and user-specific private content. Canonical privacy protection strategies typically substitute sensitive regions with surrogate content before cloud editing. Yet, the resulting output is often an edited surrogate rather than the desired edited source image, neglecting the local recovery in both design and evaluation scope. To this end, we introduce SPPE (Surrogate-based Privacy-Preserving Editing), the first recovery-oriented benchmark covering 36 fine-grained privacy categories and 65 editing instructions. It defines two complementary tasks: 1) editability assessment, which estimates before cloud interaction whether a surrogate can induce an edit consistent with the original image; and 2) surrogate-to-source edit recovery, which evaluates whether the edited surrogate can be transferred back to the private source with the edit effect preserved. We address each task with a dedicated method: ERMA predicts surrogate editability through instruction-aware multimodal relation modeling, while \method performs cycle-consistent recovery by using the surrogate editing pair as visual edit evidence and the source image as a source-preserving anchor. Experiments on SPPE and InstructPix2Pix show consistent improvements on both tasks. For editability assessment, ERMA improves over the best-performing baselines by 13.9% in SRCC and 12.3% in PLCC. For surrogate-to-source edit recovery, C2E-S2SER outperforms SOER across all 8 source integrity and edit consistency metrics on SPPE.

</details>


### [63] [Textual Supervision Enhances Geospatial Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07172)
*Marcelo Sartori Locatelli,Fernando Tonucci,Jea Kwon,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Bryan Nathanael Wijaya,Cheng Yaw Low,Virgilio Almeida,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了三种模型族（纯视觉架构、视觉-语言模型、大规模多模态基础模型）在地理空间表征学习上的表现，发现文本监督能增强地理空间编码，多模态学习是推动地理空间AI的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间理解在图像地理定位和空间推理等机器学习任务中至关重要，但目前对此维度的探索仍然不足，亟需系统分析不同模型家族的地理空间表征能力。

Method: 对三种模型族（ViT等纯视觉架构、CLIP等视觉-语言模型、LLaVA/Qwen/Gemma等多模态基础模型）进行跨图像聚类（人物、地标、日常物体）评估，根据可定位程度分组，对比分析其空间准确性。

Result: 揭示了不同模型在地理空间准确性上的系统性差距，并证明文本监督（语言模态）能够增强地理空间表征的学习。

Conclusion: 语言作为编码空间上下文的有效互补模态，多模态学习是推动地理空间AI发展的关键方向。

Abstract: Geospatial understanding is a critical yet underexplored dimension in the development of machine learning systems for tasks such as image geolocation and spatial reasoning. In this work, we analyze the geospatial representations acquired by three model families: vision-only architectures (e.g., ViT), vision-language models (e.g., CLIP), and large-scale multimodal foundation models (e.g., LLaVA, Qwen, and Gemma). By evaluating across image clusters, including people, landmarks, and everyday objects, grouped based on the degree of localizability, we reveal systematic gaps in spatial accuracy and show that textual supervision enhances the learning of geospatial representations. Our findings suggest the role of language as an effective complementary modality for encoding spatial context and multimodal learning as a key direction for advancing geospatial AI.

</details>


### [64] [Seeing Without Exposing: Adaptive Privacy Control for Open-World, Context-Hungry MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07175)
*Siyuan Xu,Yibing Liu,Peilin Chen,Yung-Hui Li,Shiqi Wang,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的隐私保护方法APD（锚定隐私漂移），通过将敏感元素漂移到语义等价的替代项同时锚定上下文线索到源图像，并构建了涵盖22个隐私类别的综合基准AdaptShield。实验证明该方法在隐私净化和内容保留上取得平衡提升，在多个MLLM系列上平均提升10.4%（文本类别）和8.5%（MLLM评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）引发了新的隐私挑战：用户输入包含不可预测的敏感信息，模型推理依赖的视觉上下文本身可能涉及隐私。现有方法依赖预定义敏感类别和固定混淆策略，难以应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出Anchored Privacy Drifting (APD)——一种无需训练的方法，将隐私敏感元素漂移到语义等价的替代项，同时将上下文线索锚定到源图像。同时构建AdaptShield基准，包含22个隐私类别，结合传统隐私指标和基于MLLM的上下文效用评估。

Result: 在Qwen2.5、Qwen3、InternVL3和InternVL3.5四个MLLM系列上，文本类别平均提升10.4%，基于MLLM的评估平均提升8.5%。

Conclusion: APD方法在隐私保护与内容保留之间实现了平衡提升，相比依赖预定义类别和固定策略的现有方法更具优势。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have raised new privacy challenges. On the data side, user-provided inputs often include unpredictable sensitive information; while on the downstream task side, model reasoning depends on rich visual context that may itself be privacy-sensitive. Existing privacy protection methods, however, rely on predefined sensitive categories and fixed obfuscation strategies, struggling to tackle such challenges in MLLMs. To address this dilemma, we propose Anchored Privacy Drifting (APD), a training-free method that drifts privacy-sensitive elements toward semantically equivalent alternatives while anchoring contextual cues to the source image. To systematically evaluate this dual objective of privacy protection and contextual preservation, we introduce AdaptShield, a comprehensive benchmark covering 22 privacy categories, which combines conventional privacy metrics with MLLM-based assessments of contextual utility. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves balanced improvements in both privacy sanitization and content retention, with average gains of 10.4% on textual categories and 8.5% under MLLM-based evaluation across four MLLM series, i.e., Qwen2.5, Qwen3, InternVL3, and InternVL3.5.

</details>


### [65] [OPTIMUS-Prime: Minimal and Sufficient Concept Explanations for Deep Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07180)
*Arthur Hoarau,Chenrui Zhu,Vu Linh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPTIMUS是一个基于概念的可视化解释框架，利用素蕴含理论为深度学习分类模型生成既满足充分性（保证预测结果）又满足最小性（无冗余特征）的视觉热力图，弥补了现有可解释AI方法在形式化保证上的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉中的XAI方法虽然面向终端用户可解释，但缺乏形式化理论保证，导致实用性与严谨性之间存在关键鸿沟。

Method: 基于素蕴含理论（prime implicants）构建概念级视觉热力图，通过数学形式化保证解释的充分性（highlighted concepts provably guarantee prediction）和最小性（no strict subset retains guarantee）。

Result: 在图像分类基准测试中验证，OPTIMUS热力图能够自然且忠实地呈现模型预测所依据的决策相关概念。

Conclusion: OPTIMUS在保持终端用户可解释性的同时，首次为基于显著图的可视化解释提供了充分性与最小性的形式化数学保证，弥合了XAI的理论-实践鸿沟。

Abstract: The growing demand for transparency in automated decision-making has propelled eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) to the forefront of machine learning research. In computer vision, however, existing explanation methods often prioritize end-user accessibility at the expense of formal guarantees, leaving a critical gap between practical utility and theoretical rigor. In this paper, we address this gap by introducing OPTIMUS, a novel framework for generating concept-based visual explanations for deep classification models. OPTIMUS explanations take the form of visual heatmaps that not only remain interpretable to end users, but are grounded in the well-established theory of prime implicants, providing formal guarantees that have been largely absent from existing saliency-based methods. Specifically, OPTIMUS explanations satisfy two desirable properties: sufficiency, ensuring that the highlighted concepts provably guarantee the classifier's prediction, and minimality, ensuring that no strict subset of those concepts retains this guarantee. Together, these properties yield explanations that are both logically tight and visually coherent. We validate our approach on a visual classification benchmark, demonstrating that OPTIMUS heatmaps naturally and faithfully surface the decision-relevant concepts underlying model predictions.

</details>


### [66] [AdaTok: Self-Budgeting Image Tokenization with Quality-Preserving Dynamic Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07185)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Yuxi Chen,Jie Zhang,Jian Liu,Jingcai Guo,Fangqi Zhu,Tao Han,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaTok 是一种自预算离散 1D 图像分词器，通过表示-分配协同设计（优先表示学习 + 自适应令牌分配），让分词器自己决定每个图像需要多少令牌，在 ImageNet-1K 上自适应平均仅用 ~118 令牌达到 rFID 1.50，相比固定 256 令牌方案提升 ~2.1 倍生成吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分词器对所有图像使用固定数量的令牌，但视觉复杂性高度异质：简单图像浪费令牌，复杂图像令牌不足。现有弹性分词器虽支持可变长度重建，但令牌长度往往作为部署时的操作点、搜索目标或外部预测，而非分词器自身的输出。

Method: 提出 AdaTok，包含两大协同设计：(1) 优先表示学习（Prioritized Representation Learning）——通过嵌套尾部掩码对令牌排序，并使用多头 LoRA 解码器头解决预算相关的语义偏移；(2) 自适应令牌分配（Adaptive Token Allocation）——训练轻量级确定性分组 GRPO 策略，在候选预算上决策。通过动态帕累托加权（Dynamic Pareto Weighting）平衡保真度与效率，无需手动权衡搜索。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上，AdaTok-Full（满预算 256 令牌）达到 rFID 1.31；AdaTok-Adaptive（自适应）平均仅使用 ~118 令牌，达到 rFID 1.50，在可比预算下优于离散 1D 基线。在自回归图像生成中，自适应表示相比固定 256 令牌解码实现 ~2.1 倍吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 视觉令牌数量可以作为内容条件化的输出被学习，而非设为固定超参数；表示-分配协同设计是实现自预算分词的关键。

Abstract: Image tokenizers, from 2D grids to recent 1D sequences, typically encode every image with the same fixed number of tokens. Yet visual complexity is highly heterogeneous, so a uniform budget overspends on simple inputs and underserves complex ones. Existing elastic tokenizers expose variable-length reconstructions, but often leave token length as a deployment-time operating point, a search target, or an external prediction rather than an output of the tokenizer itself. In this work, we ask whether a discrete visual tokenizer can budget itself in one pass. Our central finding is that actionable elasticity requires a representation--allocation co-design: prefixes must remain decodable across budgets, and the tokenizer must learn which prefix each image needs. We propose AdaTok, a self-budgeting discrete 1D tokenizer. AdaTok combines Prioritized Representation Learning, which orders tokens with nested tail masking and resolves budget-dependent semantic shift through Multi-Head LoRA decoder heads, with Adaptive Token Allocation, which trains a lightweight deterministic-group GRPO policy over candidate budgets. Dynamic Pareto Weighting balances fidelity and efficiency during policy training without manual trade-off sweeps. On ImageNet-1K, AdaTok-Full reaches rFID 1.31 at 256 tokens, while AdaTok-Adaptive attains rFID 1.50 using only ~118 tokens on average, outperforming discrete 1D baselines at comparable budgets. In autoregressive image generation, the shorter adaptive representation yields ~2.1x throughput over a fixed 256-token decode, suggesting that visual token count can be learned as a content-conditioned output rather than set as a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [67] [DualGate-Net: A Prior-Gated Dual-Encoder Framework for Histopathology Cell Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07222)
*Bahman Jafari Tabaghsar,Son Tran,K. Devaraja,Atul Sajjanhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DualGate-Net，一种先验感知的双编码器框架，通过可学习的先验门控融合机制自适应调控组织先验影响，并结合辅助前景重建分支和细胞性引导线索，在OCELOT基准上实现一致的细胞检测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理学图像中的细胞检测强烈依赖于周围组织环境，视觉上相似的细胞在不同微环境下可能属于不同类别。现有组织感知方法虽整合了上下文先验，但常依赖静态融合策略，可能传播噪声信息。

Method: 提出DualGate-Net框架：采用ConvNeXtV2局部编码器和SegFormer全局编码器的双编码器结构，通过可学习的先验门控融合机制自适应调控组织先验在空间位置上的影响；引入辅助前景重建分支以保留训练过程中的高频细胞结构；融入辅助细胞性引导线索以提升定位鲁棒性。

Result: 在OCELOT基准上验证集macro F1-score达0.7722，测试集达0.7345，实现一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 自适应先验整合策略对于鲁棒的组织病理学细胞检测是有效的，DualGate-Net通过可学习的先验门控机制优于静态融合方法。

Abstract: Cell detection in histopathology images strongly depends on surrounding tissue context, where visually similar cells may belong to different classes under different microenvironments. Recent tissue-aware methods incorporate contextual priors, but often rely on static fusion strategies that may propagate noisy information. In this work, we propose DualGate-Net, a prior-aware dual-encoder framework that combines a ConvNeXtV2-based local encoder and a SegFormer-based global encoder through a learnable prior-gated fusion mechanism. The proposed module adaptively regulates the influence of tissue priors across spatial locations, while an auxiliary foreground reconstruction branch preserves high-frequency cellular structures during training. In addition, auxiliary cellness-guided cues are incorporated to further improve localization robustness. Experiments on the OCELOT benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements, achieving macro F1-scores of 0.7722 on the validation set and 0.7345 on the test set, highlighting the effectiveness of adaptive prior integration for robust histopathology cell detection.

</details>


### [68] [Does Appearance Help? A Systematic Study of Image-Based Re-Identification in Online 3D Multi-Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07233)
*Eduardo Borges,Luís Garrote,Urbano J. Nunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了在在线3D多目标跟踪（MOT）中集成基于图像的ReID技术，提出了一种轻量级的投影框架，将几何建模与外观建模解耦，通过级联匹配策略在KITTI数据集上验证了轻量级架构能够在低延迟和强判别力之间取得最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-based 3D MOT仅依赖几何信息，在长时间遮挡或拥挤人群中难以区分目标；而融合RGB-based ReID虽能保持身份上下文，但现有方法依赖计算昂贵的并行检测器，阻碍了移动机器人的实时响应能力。

Method: 提出轻量级投影框架解耦几何与外观建模；系统分析轻量级CNN和Vision Transformer等特征提取架构；评估多种多模态数据关联策略（包括朴素线性融合和级联匹配策略）。

Result: 朴素线性融合因视觉噪声导致性能下降；级联匹配策略成功恢复被遮挡轨迹且不牺牲整体精度，有效防止身份切换；轻量级架构可在低延迟与强判别力间取得最优平衡。

Conclusion: 轻量级投影框架结合级联匹配策略能够高效实现在线3D MOT，兼顾移动机器人所需的低延迟导航和社会感知所需的判别力，适用于人机交互场景。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) typically relies solely on geometric information, which is often insufficient to distinguish between targets during prolonged occlusions or in crowded human-populated environments. While integrating RGB-based Re-Identification (ReID) offers a theoretical solution for preserving identity context, existing approaches often rely on computationally expensive parallel detectors that hinder real-time robot responsiveness. This work presents a systematic study of image-based ReID in online 3D MOT, utilizing a lightweight projection-based framework to decouple geometric and appearance modeling for mobile robots. A comprehensive analysis of feature extraction architectures is conducted, employing lightweight CNNs and Vision Transformers, and evaluating various multi-modal data association strategies to balance computational latency with robust tracking. Experiments on the Pedestrian class of the KITTI dataset reveal that naive linear fusion, of appearance and motion costs, degrades performance due to visual noise. Conversely, a cascaded matching strategy successfully recovers occluded tracks without compromising overall precision, effectively preventing identity switches to maintain human-robot interaction continuity. We show that lightweight architectures can offer an optimal trade-off between the low latency required for safe navigation and the discriminative power needed for social awareness.

</details>


### [69] [Reconstructing Multi-Decadal Forest Disturbances: A Spatio-Temporal Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07249)
*Linus Scheibenreif,Anton Raichuk,Maxim Neumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉Transformer的深度学习框架，通过同时建模时间轨迹和空间邻域，绘制了美国本土38年（1984-2022）的森林干扰图，在多卫星数据上验证了高精度（最高98.2%），但不同干扰类型间存在性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的森林干扰监测方法依赖逐像素分析卫星时间序列，忽略了空间上下文信息，导致结果缺乏空间连贯性。作者旨在通过同时考虑时间和空间信息来提高森林扰动监测的准确性和一致性。

Method: 采用视觉Transformer架构，同时建模时间轨迹和空间邻域；利用弱监督信号过滤噪声以生成空间连贯的干扰图；在Landsat、Sentinel-1、Sentinel-2等多卫星数据上，覆盖38年和近6年两个时间窗口进行评估；使用人工标注验证数据集（n=300）和独立火场边界数据集（n=706）进行性能验证。

Result: 时空模型在MTBS数据集上达到98.2%的精确度（±1年检测），在CONUS验证数据集上精确度达71.3%；F1分数分别达到75.8%和47.3%；有效减少了空间伪影；但与逐像素基线相比，在不同干扰类型间存在性能权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法为一致的森林监测提供了有前景的基础，但未来需要进一步优化以平衡不同干扰类型间的性能表现。

Abstract: Accurate monitoring of forest disturbances is essential for understanding carbon dynamics and land management, yet traditional approaches typically rely on pixel-wise analysis of satellite time-series, ignoring spatial context. We present a deep learning framework that maps 38 years (1984-2022) of forest disturbance across the contiguous United States by modeling temporal trajectories and spatial neighborhoods simultaneously. By leveraging a vision transformer architecture, our approach effectively filters noise from weak supervision signals to produce spatially coherent disturbance maps. We perform exhaustive evaluations across multiple satellites (Landsat, Sentinel-1, Sentinel-2) and temporal windows (38 years and the more recent 6 years), validating performance against a novel, manually annotated validation dataset (n=300) and independent fire perimeter dataset (n=706). The results highlight the complexity of the task: while our spatio-temporal model demonstrates high precision (up to 98.2% for +-1 year detection on MTBS and up to 71.3% on the CONUS validation datasets, with F1-scores up to 75.8% and 47.3%, respectively) and effectively reduces spatial artifacts, it exhibits performance trade-offs across different disturbance regimes compared to pixel-wise baselines. Our method offers a promising foundation for consistent forest monitoring.

</details>


### [70] [Geometric-Aware Hypergraph Reasoning for Novel Class Discovery in Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07280)
*Zihao Zhang,Aming Wu,Yang Li,Yahong Han,Jialie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超图框架和几何感知原型的方法，用于点云分割中的新类别发现。该方法通过高阶关联建模替代传统的成对关联，并引入几何信息增强类别表征，在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖成对关联进行类别分配和新类别推理，难以捕捉已知类和新类别之间的复杂关系，导致语义分割不准确。同时，现有方法过度关注语义特征提取，忽视了点云中几何信息的利用。

Method: 提出超图框架以建模类别间的高阶关联，实现从已知类别到新类别的协同推理；提出几何感知原型（Geometric-Aware Prototypes）来增强类别级几何线索的表示，并通过超边传播几何信息，改善类别空间分布的理解。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS两个数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性和优越性，实现了更精确的语义分割。

Conclusion: 通过超图框架和几何感知原型的结合，有效解决了点云新类别发现中复杂关系建模和几何信息利用不足的问题，显著提升了分割准确性。

Abstract: Novel class discovery in point cloud segmentation aims to transfer knowledge from known classes to automatically identify and segment unlabeled novel classes in point clouds. Existing methods mainly rely on pairwise associations for class assignment and novel class reasoning, which limits their ability to capture complex relationships among known and novel classes and may lead to inaccurate semantic segmentation. To address this issue, we introduce a hypergraph-based framework that models high-order associations among classes and enables collaborative reasoning from known classes to novel classes beyond traditional pairwise relations. Moreover, existing methods tend to focus on semantic feature extraction while paying insufficient attention to geometric information in point clouds. To better exploit spatial structure, we propose Geometric-Aware Prototypes to enhance the representation of class-level geometric cues. By propagating geometric information through hyperedges, the proposed method improves the understanding of spatial distributions across classes and leads to more accurate segmentation. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI and SemanticPOSS datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method.

</details>


### [71] [The Lipreading Gap: Do VSR Models Perceive Visual Speech Like Human Lipreaders?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07435)
*Rishabh Jain,Naomi Harte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了视觉语音识别（VSR）模型虽然在基准测试中超越了人类唇读能力，但其成功主要依赖于训练数据中的语言线索而非视觉感知。通过对比三种VSR系统与人类基线在MaFI数据集上的表现，发现模型与人类在单词成功/失败模式上存在差异；仅基于初始音素的文本n-gram基线即可媲美人类；模型错误更受训练词频而非视觉信息量影响；模型在人类最难辨别的视位上提升最大，对视觉清晰度的依赖远弱于人类。结论是VSR系统未能像人类一样将视觉特征绑定到有意义的单词，其核心依赖语言而非视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VSR模型在超越人类唇读准确率后，是否真正建立了类人的视觉语音感知能力。

Method: 在MaFI单词级唇读数据集上，将三种VSR系统与人类基线进行对比分析，采用单词、字符、音素和视位四个层级的评估指标，并引入文本n-gram基线（仅基于初始音素）作为对照。通过分析错误模式、词频影响、视位混淆矩阵及人机相关性来揭示模型与人类的根本差异。

Result: 模型整体准确率更高，但在单词的成功/失败模式上与人类不同；仅含少量初始音素的文本n-gram基线即可媲美人类唇读水平；模型单词级错误更由训练词频解释而非视觉信息量；模型在人类最难辨别的视位上进步最大，对视觉清晰度的依赖性远弱于人类。

Conclusion: VSR系统主要依赖训练数据中的语言线索而非视觉感知，未能将视觉特征绑定为有意义的单词，因此并未建立类人的视觉语音感知能力。

Abstract: Visual speech recognition (VSR) models now surpass human lipreaders on benchmarks, but do such gains establish human-like visual speech perception? To explore this, we compare three VSR systems with human baselines on the MaFI word-level lipreading dataset using word, character, phoneme, and viseme-level metrics. Although models achieve higher overall accuracy, they succeed and fail on different words than humans. A text-only n-gram baseline given only a few initial phonemes rivals human lipreading. VSR word-level errors are consistently better explained by training word frequency than by the visual informativeness of words. Viseme accuracies, confusion matrices and human-model correlations further show that models gain most on visemes humans find hardest, and show much weaker dependence on visual clarity. Our work demonstrates that VSR systems rely primarily on language cues from training data rather than visual perception, failing to bind visual features into meaningful words.

</details>


### [72] [ExMesh: EXplicit Mesh Reconstruction with Topology Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07288)
*Chuanjin Fan,Lifan Wu,Wenjie Chang,Hanzhi Chang,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExMesh是一个直接优化显式网格的新框架，通过集成可微优化与离散拓扑更新，并引入自适应顶点分裂合并策略及实时UV维护，实现从粗到细的网格重建，在精度、效率和网格简洁性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多视图图像重建表面网格的方法大多依赖中间表示和后处理步骤（如Marching Cubes或TSDF融合），导致伪影和碎片化几何。直接优化显式网格面临两个关键挑战：如何自适应细化网格拓扑以捕捉细节而不引入退化面；以及如何在网格结构演化时保持一致的UV坐标以实现高保真纹理映射。

Method: 提出ExMesh框架，将可微优化与离散拓扑更新相结合。具体包括自适应顶点分裂合并策略（实现从粗到细的优化）和实时UV维护（保持纹理一致性），使得离散拓扑操作能够无缝集成到连续可微优化管线中。

Result: 大量实验表明，ExMesh在精度、计算效率和网格简洁性之间达到了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: ExMesh是首个将离散拓扑操作无缝集成到连续可微优化管线中的框架，有效解决了直接优化显式网格的两大关键挑战，实现了高质量的表面网格重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing surface meshes from multi-view images has remained a core challenge in recent years. Most existing methods, whether implicit or explicit, depend on intermediate representations and post-processing steps like Marching Cubes or TSDF fusion, often resulting in artifacts and fragmented geometry. Directly optimizing explicit meshes is a promising approach. However, it presents two critical challenges. The first is how to adaptively refine mesh topology to capture detail without introducing degenerate faces. The second is how to maintain consistent UV coordinates for high-fidelity texturing as the mesh structure evolves. To overcome these, we propose ExMesh, a novel framework that directly optimizes explicit meshes by integrating differentiable optimization with discrete topology updates. Specifically, we introduce an adaptive vertex splitting and merging strategy, along with real-time UV maintenance, to enable coarse-to-fine optimization while preserving geometric integrity. To our knowledge, ExMesh is the first framework to seamlessly integrate discrete topology operations into a continuous differentiable optimization pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ExMesh achieves a balance among accuracy, computational efficiency, and mesh conciseness.

</details>


### [73] [TEVI: Text-Conditioned Editing of Visual Representations via Sparse Autoencoders for Improved Vision-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07451)
*Sweta Mahajan,Sukrut Rao,Jiahao Xie,Alexander Koller,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TEVI提出利用稀疏自编码器解耦图像嵌入，并通过基于文本描述的训练掩码模块选择性重建嵌入，从而解决CLIP模型中图像与文本嵌入对齐不佳的问题，在多个检索基准上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型虽然拥有共享的图像-文本嵌入空间，但图像与文本嵌入之间往往对齐不佳，影响下游任务性能。近期研究表明，这源于信息不平衡：图像包含的信息远超其对应的文本描述。

Method: 提出TEVI框架：1）使用稀疏自编码器对图像嵌入进行解耦；2）训练一个掩码模块，根据给定的文本描述信号，选择性重建图像嵌入中与描述相关的部分，同时丢弃无关信息。

Result: 1）在合成文本控制的实验中，TEVI能有效保留描述所涉及的属性并丢弃其他属性；2）在自然图像上应用后，在粗粒度短文本基准（MS COCO, Flickr）和细粒度长文本基准（IIW, DOCCI）上均取得更优的检索性能，且对更丰富的文本描述增益更强；3）在RoCOCO鲁棒性基准上表现更优。

Conclusion: TEVI通过利用文本描述作为信号指导图像嵌入的选择性保留，有效缓解了图像-文本嵌入对齐中的信息不平衡问题，尤其在文本描述更丰富时效果更为显著。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP are highly useful for diverse tasks due to their shared image-text embedding space. Despite this, the image and text embeddings are often poorly aligned, affecting downstream performance. Recent work has shown that this can be attributed to an information imbalance: images contain more information than their captions describe. In this work, we propose TEVI, a framework that uses captions as a signal for what to retain from image embeddings. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders to disentangle image embeddings and train a masking module to selectively reconstruct the embedding based on a given caption. In a controlled setup with synthetic captions, we show that TEVI is effective at preserving caption-described attributes while discarding others.
