TL;DR: 提出了一种严重性感知的多模型框架，结合课程训练策略与相关性响应选择，在MAQA数据集上通过BERTScore评估达到90.30%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在不同严重程度的医疗查询中难以提供一致且上下文适当的响应，需要能够适应医疗查询渐进复杂性的模型。

Method: 采用三阶段课程学习策略，依次在轻度、中度和危重病例上顺序训练五个独立的大语言模型；推理时所有模型生成候选响应，选择最合适的作为最终输出。

Result: 在MAQA数据集上，基线设置达86.71%的BERTScore，微调后达90.30%，优于基线和微调模型。

Conclusion: 课程学习与多模型响应选择的结合有效提升了医疗文本生成中的响应质量和相关性。

Abstract: Telehealth systems have become increasingly important for delivering accessible and timely medical information. Existing large language models often struggle to provide consistent and contextually appropriate medical responses across varying levels of case severity. This limitation highlights the need for models that can effectively adapt to the progressive complexity in medical queries. To address this challenge, we introduce a severity-aware multi-model framework that integrates curriculum training strategy with relevance-based response selection. The proposed framework employs a three-stage curriculum learning strategy, where each model is trained sequentially on mild, moderate, and critical cases to progressively acquire domain knowledge. The approach utilizes five large language models, each independently trained under the same curriculum scheme. During inference, all models generate candidate responses, and the most appropriate response is selected as the final output. The framework is trained and evaluated on the MAQA dataset, which provides annotated medical question-answer pairs. Experimental results evaluated using BERTScore demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance compared to both baseline and fine-tuned models, attaining 86.71% in the baseline setting and 90.30% after fine-tuning. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining curriculum learning with multi-model response selection in improving response quality and relevance in medical text generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Elmes*: Automated Construction of Fine-Grained Evaluation Rubrics for Large Language Models in Long-Tail Educational Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06546)
*Tao Liu,Ye Lu,Ruohua Zhang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Elmes*框架，构建细粒度场景化评估标准（Edu-330含330个场景），揭示教育场景中LLM能力的多维性（创造力、价值观整合、苏格拉底式引导等），并验证LLM评委与人类评分的一致性及偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重于通用正确性或依赖人工设计的评估标准，难以扩展到长尾教学场景，缺乏对LLM教学能力（而非仅知识）的系统评估。

Method: 结合声明式多智能体引擎（教师-学生-评委交互）与SceneGen自进化模块，从专家定义的教学维度协同优化评估标准与测试数据，构建含330个场景、11门学科、3个年级段的Edu-330基准。

Result: 顶级LLM差异主要在创造力与价值观整合；知识强的模型可能在苏格拉底式引导上失败；教育专用模型InnoSpark获得最佳人工评分；LLM评委保持人类可比排名但评分方差更低，存在自偏好等偏差；少样本锚定改进人机对齐，推理强制与贪心解码依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: Elmes*为基于教学法的LLM评估提供了可扩展的诊断基础设施，揭示了教育能力评估的多维性需求。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for education requires measuring how models teach, not only what they know. Existing benchmarks emphasize domain-general correctness or depend on manually designed rubrics that scale poorly to long-tail pedagogical scenarios. We introduce Elmes*, an end-to-end framework for constructing, refining, and applying fine-grained scenario-specific rubrics. Elmes* combines a declarative multi-agent engine for teacher--student--judge interactions with SceneGen, a self-evolving module that co-optimizes evaluation criteria and test data from expert-defined pedagogical dimensions. Using Elmes*, we build Edu-330, covering 330 scenarios across 11 subjects, 3 grade bands, and 10 task types, with over 1{,}000 second-level indicators. Experiments on Edu-330 and four expert-authored gold-standard scenarios show that educational capability is multidimensional: top-tier LLMs differ mainly in creativity and values integration, knowledge-strong models may fail at Socratic scaffolding, and the education-specialized InnoSpark achieves the best human-evaluated average score. LLM judges preserve human-comparable rankings with much lower scoring variance, but exhibit judge-specific biases such as self-preference. Ablations show that expert-scored few-shot anchoring improves human--LLM alignment, while reasoning enforcement and greedy decoding are model-dependent. Elmes* thus provides scalable diagnostic infrastructure for pedagogically grounded LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [190] [FAIR-Calib: Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration for Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06547)
*Haoyu Huang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Boyu Liu,Guodong Guo,Zhongqian Fu,Hang Zhou,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在迭代细化过程中早期决策易被PTQ量化误差翻转并永久锁定的问题，提出FAIR-Calib两阶段后训练量化框架：第一阶段估计位置先验，第二阶段通过重加权隐状态MSE进行离策略逐层校准，在无需完整扩散过程的情况下有效保护脆弱的前沿状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在迭代式token细化过程中存在"稳定性滞后"问题——早期做出的边界决策在写入后仍很脆弱。后训练量化（PTQ）误差极易在写入前沿翻转这些边界决策，且一旦被翻转就被永久锁定并放大，严重损害模型性能。

Method: 提出FAIR-Calib两阶段PTQ框架。阶段I：在全精度教师模型上探测，估计结合前沿命中（frontier hits）和掩码阶段可靠性的位置先验。阶段II：进行离策略、逐层校准，最小化重加权后的隐状态均方误差（MSE），优先保护脆弱的前沿状态，且无需昂贵的端到端扩散 rollout。理论层面证明该加权目标函数是输出KL散度的有效代理。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型上（W4A4设定）一致性地超越现有基线方法，显著减少了前沿决策翻转次数，并在多个基准上抑制了提交后的不匹配。

Conclusion: FAIR-Calib通过两阶段框架有效解决了扩散LLM中PTQ导致的前沿决策稳定性问题，在不引入额外推理开销的前提下实现了更好的量化性能，为扩散语言模型的部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) refine tokens iteratively but commit them irreversibly, leading to a "stability lag" where early decisions remain fragile even after being written. We reveal that Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) error easily flips these borderline decisions at the write frontier, which are then permanently locked in and amplified. To address this, we propose Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration (FAIR-Calib), a two-stage PTQ framework for dLLMs. Stage I probes a full-precision teacher to estimate a position prior that combines frontier hits and masked-stage reliability. Stage II performs off-policy, layer-wise calibration by minimizing a reweighted hidden-state MSE, effectively prioritizing the protection of fragile frontier states without requiring expensive end-to-end diffusion rollouts. We further theoretically justify our weighted objective as a surrogate for output KL divergence. Empirically, FAIR-Calib consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on LLaDA and Dream (W4A4), significantly reducing frontier decision flips and suppressing post-commit mismatches across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification using THz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554)
*Roshni Mahtani,Ilán Carretero,Laura Monroy,Aldo Moreno-Oyervides,Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique,Rocío del Amor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MSFAN深度学习架构结合太赫兹双梳光谱技术，实现对12种聚合物的分类，准确率达85.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分类和光谱技术难以稳健区分回收塑料中的聚合物，亟需更有效的识别方法。

Method: 构建多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），集成特征门控信号重校准、多尺度并行卷积、交叉特征注意力和注意力池化，用于处理THz-DCS光谱数据。

Result: 在12种聚合物（包括纯聚合物、多层膜、商业共混物和生物聚合物）分类任务中达到85.2%的准确率，优于现有模型。

Conclusion: 结合THz-DCS与深度学习技术能够实现有效、可扩展且可解释的聚合物分类，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable polymer identification is essential for ensuring the quality and safety of recycled plastics, yet conventional sorting and spectroscopic techniques often struggle to deliver robust discrimination. Terahertz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy (THz-DCS) offers a promising alternative, providing rapid, high-resolution, and non-destructive measurements. In this work, we leverage THz-DCS to classify 12 types of polymers, including pure polymers, multilayer films, commercial blends, and biopolymers. To handle the complexity of these spectral signals, we propose the Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network (MSFAN), a novel deep learning architecture tailored for THz-DCS data. The framework integrates feature gating for signal recalibration and multi-scale parallel convolutions to capture diverse frequency patterns. These features are further refined through cross-feature attention and attention pooling, enabling the model to intrinsically highlight the most informative THz regions. MSFAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, reaching a classification accuracy of 85.2%. This study demonstrates the potential of combining THz-DCS with deep learning techniques for effective, scalable, and interpretable polymer classification.

</details>


### [192] [MacArena: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on an Online macOS Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06560)
*Victor Muryn,Maksym Shamrai,Sofiia Mazepa,Yehor Khodysko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacArena是一个针对macOS平台的GUI代理基准测试，包含421个跨50个应用的人工验证任务，揭示了现有模型在macOS上的表现远不如Linux基准测试，排名甚至出现逆转，表明当前GUI代理缺乏真正的跨平台能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: macOS在GUI代理基准测试中严重缺失，现有基准（如macOSWorld）仅覆盖少量原生应用且运行在x86虚拟机上，与Apple Silicon不兼容，无法有效评估模型在macOS上的真实能力。

Method: 构建MacArena基准，整合了OSWorld任务移植、macOSWorld内容以及49个全新的macOS原生任务，总计421个任务覆盖50个应用，全部运行在Apple Silicon的原生虚拟化框架上。

Result: 模型在现有Linux基准上的表现可能反映的是对任务分布的熟悉程度而非真正的跨平台GUI能力；模型排名在移植任务和macOS原生任务之间出现反转，领先模型在MacArena子集上落后超过26%。

Conclusion: macOS对当前GUI代理构成了一个真正更困难的环境，现有基准无法有效评估跨平台GUI能力，MacArena填补了这一空白并揭示了模型能力的局限性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate graphical user interfaces (GUIs) through vision and control primitives, and their capabilities have advanced rapidly, driven in part by standardized online evaluation benchmarks such as OSWorld, which serve both as evaluation tools and as training environments for reinforcement learning. However, macOS remains underserved in this landscape: the only existing benchmark, macOSWorld, covers a narrow slice of first-party applications with simpler tasks, and runs on x86 virtual machines incompatible with Apple Silicon. We introduce MacArena, a benchmark of 421 manually verified tasks spanning 50 applications that combines a curated port of OSWorld tasks, content sourced from macOSWorld, and 49 new macOS-native tasks, all running on Apple's native Virtualization framework on Apple Silicon. We argue that macOS presents distinct GUI challenges beyond what Linux-based benchmarks capture, and our evaluation supports this claim: strong model performance on existing benchmarks can reflect familiarity with task distributions rather than genuine cross-platform GUI competence. Notably, model rankings invert between ported and macOS-native tasks, with a leading model trailing by over 26% on the MacArena subset, suggesting that macOS poses a genuinely harder environment for current GUI agents.

</details>


### [193] [WAV: Multi-Resolution Block Residual Routing for Deep Decoder-Only Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06564)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAV v1是一种轻量级多分辨率残差路由方法，通过为每个Transformer块引入方向性细节基（相位基和分割基）来增强块级残差摘要，在深层解码器（48层）上显著优于现有残差路由方法，验证了方向性残差细节对深度缩放的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PreNorm残差流使用固定单位权重累积子层更新，而现有Attention Residuals和Block Attention Residuals方法虽然实现了内容感知的深度路由，但仅存储块内低频总残差位移，丢失了注意力与MLP的不平衡、块内早期与后期子层更新的方向性结构信息。

Method: 提出WAV v1，为每个Transformer块增加两个方向性细节基：相位基（对比注意力和MLP更新）和分割基（对比早期和后期子层更新）。这些基与标准块摘要通过相同的深度softmax混合器进行路由，并通过负细节源初始化和分离的RMS匹配稳定训练。

Result: 在字符级TinyStories和Text8语言建模任务中，WAV v1在12层时并非一致有效，但在24层时具有竞争力，48层时全面超越所有基线。具体地，在48层时，TinyStories验证损失从Block AttnRes的0.4960降至0.4738，Text8从0.9363降至0.9305，且参数量增加可忽略。

Conclusion: 方向性残差细节（而不仅仅是块级总和）对于深度Transformer中残差路由的缩放至关重要，WAV v1通过轻量级多分辨率路由有效捕捉了这些信息。

Abstract: Residual connections are central to training deep Transformers, but standard PreNorm residual streams aggregate sublayer updates with fixed unit weights. Recent Attention Residuals replace this fixed accumulation with content-dependent depth-wise routing, and Block Attention Residuals make the mechanism efficient by routing over block-level residual summaries. However, a single block summary stores only the low-frequency total residual displacement inside a block, discarding directional structure such as attention-vs-MLP imbalance and early-vs-late block dynamics. We propose WAV v1, a lightweight multi-resolution residual routing method for decoder-only Transformers. Instead of representing each block only by its accumulated residual sum, WAV v1 augments every block with two directional detail bases: a phase basis that contrasts attention and MLP updates, and a split basis that contrasts early and late sublayer updates. These bases are routed together with standard block summaries through the same depth-wise softmax mixer, while negative detail-source initialization and detached RMS matching stabilize training. On character-level TinyStories and Text8 language modeling, WAV v1 shows a clear depth-dependent benefit. Although it is not consistently beneficial at 12 layers, it becomes competitive at 24 layers and outperforms all baselines at 48 layers. At 48 layers, WAV v1 reduces validation loss relative to Block AttnRes from 0.4960 to 0.4738 on TinyStories and from 0.9363 to 0.9305 on Text8, with negligible additional parameters. These results suggest that directional residual details, not only block-level sums, are important for scaling residual routing in deeper Transformers.

</details>


### [194] [Are you sure? A Comprehensive and Comprehensible Survey of Uncertainty Quantification in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06567)
*Julia Reuter,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了符号回归（SR）中的不确定性量化（UQ）问题，首次系统性地介绍了UQ的基本概念，并将现有研究归纳为频率学派、贝叶斯和模型选择三大方向，指出SR中的UQ仍是一个未充分探索的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归在现实决策中的应用受到缺乏不确定性量化支持的制约；不确定性量化能提供模型可靠性信息，有助于避免过拟合并为决策提供依据。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述的方式，梳理了符号回归中不确定性量化的三个研究方向：频率学派方法、贝叶斯方法以及模型选择方法。

Result: 识别并归类了现有符号回归中的不确定性量化文献为三大研究方向，同时指出该领域整体上仍处于未充分探索的状态。

Conclusion: 符号回归中的不确定性量化是一个重要但尚未被充分研究的问题，需要进一步开发可靠的UQ方法以推动SR在实际决策中的应用。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is a class of methods that systematically explore the space of mathematical functions to discover models that accurately capture the underlying relationships in a dataset. Despite recent advances in the field, a lack of support for uncertainty quantification (UQ) limits its adoption in real-world decision processes. In regression analysis, UQ provides important information about the model reliability, which can both help to avoid overfitting by accounting for uncertainty in the data, and provide insights for decision-making. This survey is the first to clearly address this issue, with the objective of introducing essential UQ concepts and reviewing the current literature on UQ in SR, which can be broadly organized into three research directions: frequentist, Bayesian, and model selection. Despite its importance, UQ in SR is still underexplored, which motivates further research into reliable UQ methods for SR.

</details>


### [195] [Generative Models Erode Human Temporal Learning Through Market Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06572)
*Wenjun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现代生成模型在低于AGI能力水平时，通过表面特征模仿人类时间学习（HTL）产出，导致验证成本高于收益，最终引发价值崩溃——验证侵蚀使人类深度学习投入在经济上不可持续，且对齐成功反而加剧此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示生成模型在未达到通用人工智能（AGI）水平时，如何通过对人类时间学习（HTL）产出表面特征的模仿，系统性破坏知识与文化生产的经济基础与验证机制。

Method: 提出人类时间学习（HTL）概念与价值崩溃理论路径，运用昂贵检验框架（costly-inspection framework）形式化分析，并跨领域（学术出版、法律实践、内容平台、软件安全）实证映射四个验证侵蚀阶段。

Result: 验证在经济学上失去合理性后，评估者不再区分产出模式，导致投入多年学习的生产者无法与近乎零成本的生成产出竞争；对齐成功反而缩小人机输出差距，使来源验证更困难，加剧HTL密集型工作的竞争压力。

Conclusion: 即使生成模型能力有限且对齐成功，仍会通过验证侵蚀路径引发价值崩溃，威胁人类知识积累与文化生产的可持续性。

Abstract: We argue that modern generative models create structural risks for knowledge and cultural production at current, sub-AGI capability levels. We define Human Temporal Learning (HTL) as path-dependent knowledge accumulation through sustained engagement with problems over time. Generative outputs increasingly resemble HTL-intensive work in surface features, so verifying whether a given output reflects genuine human learning grows costly relative to its expected benefit. Once verification loses economic justification, evaluators reward outputs regardless of production mode, and producers who invested years of learning compete on price against outputs that cost almost nothing to generate. We call this pathway value collapse and formalize it through a costly-inspection framework. Cross-domain evidence from academic publishing, legal practice, content platforms, and software security maps onto four stages of verification erosion. Alignment success is orthogonal. Better-aligned models narrow observable gaps between human and AI outputs, making source verification harder and intensifying competitive pressure against HTL-intensive work even when individual AI outputs improve.

</details>


### [196] [Skip a Layer or Loop It? Learning Program-of-Layers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06574)
*Ziyue Li,Yang Li,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型（LLM）中的预训练层可以像模块一样动态跳过或循环，形成针对每个输入的定制化程序（PoLar）。通过轻量级预测网络学习生成执行程序，在数学推理任务上，PoLar 能在执行更少层的情况下提高准确性，并超越标准推理和先前的动态深度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示 LLM 推理中存在训练无关的、灵活的、动态的层程序（PoLar），表明推理支持超出标准前向传播的多种有效潜在计算，从而挖掘 LLM 的潜在推理能力。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的 PoLar 预测网络，该网络学习为每个输入生成执行程序，通过动态跳过或重复预训练层来实现更高效或更准确的推理。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，PoLar 一致性地提高了准确性，优于标准推理和先前的动态深度方法，并且常常在执行更少层的情况下达到更好效果，这些增益在分布外评估中依然保持。

Conclusion: 固定深度执行仅捕获了 LLM 潜在推理能力的一小部分，而 PoLar 通过动态层程序能够更充分地利用模型能力，表明推理存在多种有效的潜在计算路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform inference by following a fixed depth and order, non-recurrent execution of all layers. We reveal the wide existence of training-free, flexible, dynamic program-of-layers (PoLar), where pretrained layers can be packed as modules and then skipped or looped to form a customized program for each input. For most inputs, substantially shorter program executions can achieve the same or better accuracy, while incorrect predictions of the original LLM can be corrected by alternative programs with fewer layers. These observations indicate that inference admits multiple valid latent computations beyond the standard forward pass. To efficiently achieve PoLar in practice, we propose a lightweight PoLar prediction network, which learns to generate execution programs that dynamically skip or repeat pretrained layers for each input. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PoLar consistently improves accuracy over standard inference and prior dynamic-depth methods, often while executing fewer layers, and that these gains persist under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results suggest that fixed-depth execution captures only a narrow subset of an LLM's latent reasoning capacity.

</details>


### [197] [Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression for Low-Data, High-Dimensional Output Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06576)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Eric T. Wolf,Mei Ting Mak,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）的模型，该模型通过将每个输出表示为从高斯过程先验中采样的低维潜在状态的线性-高斯解码，将压缩和预测耦合在单一目标函数中，从而有效处理高维输出的回归任务，并在系外行星全球气候模型的空间分辨率仿真中展示了其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学领域中，回归任务常需从少量训练样本预测高维输出。多输出高斯过程在低数据量场景下表现优异，但难以处理高维输出；而PCA-GP等压缩-预测流水线虽能处理高维数据，但其基函数是针对重构而非预测优化的，存在本质缺陷。

Method: 提出高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）模型，将每个输出表示为从高斯过程先验中采样的低维潜在状态的线性-高斯解码，并通过解析方法边缘化解码器权重，从而将压缩与预测耦合在单一目标函数中，实现可扩展至高维输出的联合优化。

Result: 首次构建了系外行星全球气候模型的空间分辨率仿真器，证明了GPLFR在高维输出回归任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: GPLFR通过联合优化压缩与预测，克服了传统方法在重构与预测目标不一致的问题，为高维输出回归提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In the sciences, regression tasks often require predicting high-dimensional outputs from few training examples. Multi-output Gaussian processes excel in low-data regimes but typically struggle with high-dimensional outputs. Compress-then-predict pipelines such as PCA-GP (principal component analysis plus Gaussian process regression) handle high dimensionality, but rely on bases optimized for reconstruction rather than prediction. To address this gap, we propose a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state drawn from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, we couple compression and prediction in a single objective that scales to high-dimensional outputs. We refer to this model as Gaussian process latent factor regression (GPLFR). We demonstrate GPLFR by building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets.

</details>


### [198] [Principles and Practice of Deep Representation Learning: or a Mathematical Theory of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06624)
*San Buchanan,Druv Pai,Peng Wang,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书旨在通过表征学习的视角，打开大型深度神经网络的"黑箱"，利用优化和信息论解释其设计原理，并提出高效、可解释且可控的新型模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型（尤其是生成模型）规模巨大，但内部机制不透明，导致可解释性、可靠性和可控性差，进而引发炒作和恐惧。

Method: 本书通过表征学习的核心视角，运用优化理论和信息论，将现代神经网络架构的设计原则简化为本科水平的线性代数和微积分练习，并在此基础上提出新的模型构建方法。

Result: 提出了新的方法和模型，这些模型在设计上即具备高效、可解释和可控的特性，其性能不逊于甚至超越传统的黑箱模型。

Conclusion: 本书为理解大型深度网络提供了系统框架，强调了表征学习在其中的核心作用，并为未来深度学习的发展指明了方向。

Abstract: In the current era of deep learning and especially generative models, there is significant investment in training very large generative models. Thus far, such models have been "black boxes" that are difficult to understand in the sense that they have opaque internal mechanisms, leading to difficulties in interpretability, reliability, and control. Naturally, this lack of understanding has led to both hype and fear.
  This book is an attempt to "open the black box" and understand the mechanisms of large deep networks, through the perspective of representation learning, which is a major factor - arguably the single most important one - in the empirical power of deep learning models. A brief outline of this book is as follows. Chapter 1 will summarize the threads that underlie the whole text. Chapters 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6 will explain the design principles of modern neural network architectures through optimization and information theory, reducing the process of architecture development (long having been described as a sort of "alchemy") to undergraduate-level linear algebra and calculus exercises once the underlying principles are introduced. Chapters 7 and 8 will discuss applications of these principles to solve problems in more paradigmatic ways, obtaining new methods and models which are efficient, interpretable, and controllable by design, and yet no less - sometimes even more - powerful than the black-box models they resemble. Chapter 9 will discuss potential future directions for deep learning, the role of representation learning, as well as some open problems.

</details>


### [199] [The Identity Trap in EEG Foundation Models: A Diagnostic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06647)
*Jun-You Lin,Ying Choon Wu,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"身份陷阱"概念，指EEG基础模型在受试者分离交叉验证中可能学习受试者身份特征而非临床生物标志物。提出FMScope协议（包含方差分解、受试者轴擦除、周期1/f消融、逐层标签探针、受试者内方向一致性五种诊断工具），在三个预训练FM和四个数据集上验证。结果表明身份陷阱普遍存在（冻结受试者方差为随机零的13-89倍），通过擦除可提升标签解码性能，而微调仅在已有文献跨受试者标记的细胞中放大标签方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示EEG基础模型在受试者分离交叉验证中高精度的歧义性——可能反映真实临床生物标志物，也可能反映与标签相关的受试者身份特征，并探讨能否在微调前从表征层面诊断此问题。

Method: 提出FMScope协议，包含五种诊断工具：方差分解、受试者轴擦除、周期1/f消融、逐层标签探针、受试者内方向一致性。应用于三个预训练模型（LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE）和四个数据集（2×2布局：标签受试者关系×是否存在共识跨受试者EEG标记）。

Result: (i)身份陷阱普遍存在：冻结受试者方差是随机零的13-89倍（12/12对），微调后全部上升（+10至+63 pp）；擦除可提升标签解码性能（受试者内标签变化+6至+12 pp，外部队列+4至+27 pp）。(ii)周期1/f是受试者载体之一：去除后LaBraM和CBraMod受试者探针下降9-19 pp，REVE无此依赖。(iii)微调仅在已有文献跨受试者标记的细胞中放大标签方差。

Conclusion: 身份陷阱是物理基础的捷径学习实例：偏好线索具有可测量的生理成分，仅靠受试者分离交叉验证无法排除。FMScope可将反映生物标记的增益与反映受试者身份的增益分离开来。

Abstract: Objective. EEG foundation models (FMs) report strong accuracy on clinical resting-state EEG. However, high accuracy under subject-disjoint cross-validation remains ambiguous: it can reflect a genuine clinical biomarker, or subject-identity features that correlate with the label. We name this the Identity Trap and ask whether it can be diagnosed at the representation level before fine-tuning.
  Approach. We propose FMScope, a frozen-representation protocol packaging five diagnostics: variance decomposition, subject-axis erasure, aperiodic 1/f ablation, layer-wise label probing, and within-subject direction consistency. We apply it to three pretrained FMs (LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE) across four datasets in a 2x2 layout: subject relation of label x presence of a consensus cross-subject EEG marker.
  Main results. (i) The Identity Trap is universal: frozen subject-variance is 13-89x a random null in 12/12 pairs, rising in all 12 under fine-tuning (+10 to +63 pp). This dominance is a removable linear axis: erasing it improves label decoding where the label varies within subject (+6 to +12 pp in primary cells; +4 to +27 pp across external cohorts). (ii) Aperiodic 1/f is one subject carrier: removing it drops the subject probe by 9-19 pp on LaBraM and CBraMod. REVE saturates subject identity without measurable aperiodic dependence. (iii) Fine-tuning amplifies label-variance only in cells with a literature-established cross-subject marker.
  Significance. The Identity Trap is a physically-grounded instance of shortcut learning: the preferred cue has a measurable physiological component, and subject-disjoint splitting alone cannot rule it out. FMScope separates gains reflecting a biological marker from those reflecting subject identity.

</details>


### [200] [Capturing non-Markovian dynamics in non-equilibrium stochastic systems using flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06658)
*Bhargav Sriram Siddani,John B. Bell,Alejandro L. Garcia,Ishan Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对粗粒化随机偏微分方程（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）无法准确捕捉短时间非马尔可夫效应和低粒子密度非高斯分布的问题，提出了一种生成流匹配方法，直接对粒子模拟中的通量概率分布进行建模，显式纳入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。在Kramers首次通过时间问题上的实验表明，该方法准确捕捉了短时行为，并在数密度统计矩预测上优于马尔可夫基线模型（正则化DK方程）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粗粒化流体动力学模型（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）基于马尔可夫近似，在短时间尺度上忽略了非马尔可夫效应，同时在低粒子密度区域无法处理高度非高斯的分布特性，导致动力学描述不准确。因此需要一种能显式纳入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应的建模方法。

Method: 开发了一种生成流匹配（generative flow matching）方法，直接对粒子模拟中的通量概率分布进行建模，通过流匹配技术学习从粒子模拟数据到目标概率分布的映射，从而自然地融合非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。

Result: 在非相互作用的布朗粒子系统的Kramers首次通过时间问题上进行验证，模型准确捕捉了短时间行为，在数密度的统计矩预测上显著优于正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程这一马尔可夫基线模型。

Conclusion: 生成流匹配方法能有效弥补传统粗粒化SPDE模型在短时间动力学和低密度非高斯区域上的不足，为随机粒子系统的粗粒化建模提供了更精确的替代方案。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic models of stochastic particle systems represented by coarse-grained stochastic partial differential equations (SPDE), such as the regularized Dean-Kawasaki (DK) equation, do not accurately capture the short-time system dynamics that is dominated by non-Markovian effects, and low particle density regimes where the distributions are highly non-Gaussian. We develop a generative flow matching method that directly models the probability distribution of fluxes from particle simulations that explicitly incorporates non-Markovian and non-Gaussian effects. As a demonstration, we use this method to simulate the Kramers first passage time problem for a system of non-interacting Brownian particles. We show the model accurately captures the short-time behavior and provides better predictions of the statistical moments of the number density when compared against the solution of the Markovian baseline, regularized DK equation.

</details>


### [201] [Explainable Runtime Dependency Tracking for AI-RAN Conflict Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06663)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级监控原语，通过布尔矩阵追踪参数-KPI依赖关系，利用滑动窗口推理在流式遥测事件中检测结构变化，为AI-RAN中的冲突诊断提供可解释信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN中多个xApp/rApp共享参数和KPI可能导致冲突，现有监控仅知道哪些应用被部署是不够的，还需要验证运行时诊断所依赖的参数-KPI关系在当前操作模式下是否仍然有效。

Method: 使用布尔矩阵表示活跃依赖关系，通过布尔矩阵乘法检查参数活动事件和KPI响应事件的一致性；提出滑动窗口推理过程，在依赖一致时复用当前估计，在观测到结构变化时重新计算。

Result: 在受控布尔事件流上的实验表明，该方法能够在依赖变化和布尔观测噪声下实现高效且准确的追踪。

Conclusion: 该追踪器旨在作为冲突诊断和慢循环模型刷新的可解释信号，而非自主缓解机制，为AI-RAN中的运行时监控提供了一种轻量级且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Future AI-integrated Radio Access Networks (AI-RAN) will combine open programmability with learning-enabled xApps, rApps, and control functions that act on shared parameters and key performance indicators (KPIs). For conflict monitoring, it is not enough to know which applications are deployed; the system must also know whether the parameter--KPI dependencies assumed by runtime diagnosis remain valid under the current operating regime. This paper studies a lightweight monitoring primitive for that purpose: tracking an interpretable dependency representation from streaming telemetry events.
  We represent active dependencies by a Boolean matrix and use Boolean matrix multiplication to check whether recent parameter-activity and KPI-response events are consistent with the current estimate. We propose a sliding-window inference procedure that reuses the estimate when it remains consistent and recomputes it when recent observations indicate structural change. The tracker is intended as an explainable signal for conflict diagnosis and slow-loop model refresh, not as an autonomous mitigation mechanism. Experiments on controlled Boolean event streams show efficient and accurate tracking under dependency changes and Boolean observation noise.

</details>


### [202] [Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06673)
*Ujjwal Bhatta,Utsabi Dangol,Sumaly Bajracharya,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ULPS通过结合A*符号轨迹、微调BERT语言模型和基于MC Dropout的不确定性感知控制，在稀疏奖励和异构任务序列的强化学习中实现了更优的收敛速度、泛化能力和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在稀疏奖励和异构任务序列中面临收敛慢、泛化弱和探索低效的挑战，亟需引入结构化的先验知识来引导策略学习。

Method: 提出ULPS框架：1）使用A*规划器合成最优符号轨迹微调BERT语言模型；2）通过MC Dropout估计认知不确定性；3）采用熵基混合机制自适应平衡LLM引导与PPO策略。

Result: 在MiniGridUnlockPickup基准上，ULPS相比无引导、未校准和标准RL基线，在执行准确率上提升超过9%，减少了环境交互次数，获得了更高的奖励AUC。

Conclusion: 集成符号A*轨迹、预训练语言先验和不确定性感知控制为多任务稀疏奖励强化学习提供了一种原则性且有效的方法，并可扩展到部分可观和多智能体场景。

Abstract: Sparse rewards and heterogeneous task sequences remain persistent challenges in Reinforcement Learning (RL), often resulting in slow convergence, weak generalization, and inefficient exploration. We propose Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping (ULPS), a novel framework that integrates a calibrated Large Language Model (LLM) into the RL training loop to provide structured, uncertainty-modulated behavioral guidance. ULPS employs an A*-based oracle to synthesize optimal symbolic trajectories, which are used to fine-tune a BERT-based language model. During training, this model supplies action suggestions whose influence is conditioned on epistemic uncertainty estimated via Monte Carlo (MC) dropout. An entropy-based blending mechanism adaptively balances LLM guidance and the learned policy (via Proximal Policy Optimization, PPO), allowing the agent to prioritize reliable priors while preserving adaptability. We evaluate ULPS on the MiniGridUnlockPickup benchmark and observe consistent improvements in success rate, reward efficiency, and sample complexity over unguided, uncalibrated, and standard RL baselines. ULPS achieves more than 9% improvement in execution accuracy after fine-tuning, requires fewer environment interactions, and yields higher reward AUC. Our results demonstrate that integrating symbolic A* trajectories, pretrained language priors, and uncertainty-aware control offers a principled and effective approach to multi-task reinforcement learning in sparse-reward domains, with potential extensibility to partially observable and multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [203] [Spatiotemporal Imputation with Graph-Informed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06682)
*Zepeng Zhang,Aref Einizade,Jhony H. Giraldo,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GiFlow框架，用图信息先验替代高斯先验，通过时空滤波构建源分布，结合混合向量场模型（空间注意力、时间注意力、时空传播）进行时空数据插补，在合成和真实数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决时空系统中缺失数据插补问题，现有方法（RNN/GNN）存在误差累积，扩散方法依赖问题无关高斯先验且需迭代采样，效率与效果受限。

Method: 1）用时空滤波构建图信息先验替代高斯先验，简化生成轨迹；2）设计混合向量场模型，融合空间注意力、时间注意力和时空传播机制。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，GiFlow优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: GiFlow通过图信息先验和混合向量场模型，显著提升时空数据插补性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Missing data is a common challenge in spatiotemporal systems, arising in applications such as air quality monitoring and urban traffic management. Traditional machine learning approaches, like recurrent and graph neural networks, rely on iterative propagation, which tends to accumulate errors over time and space. Recent diffusion-based methods mitigate error propagation but require iterative sampling and often depend on problem-agnostic Gaussian priors, limiting both efficiency and effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose GiFlow, a Graph-Informed Flow Matching framework for spatiotemporal imputation. GiFlow replaces the typical Gaussian prior with a graph-informed prior constructed via spatiotemporal filtering of observable signals, which better aligns the source distribution to the target and thereby simplifies the generation trajectory. The flow field is parameterized by a hybrid vector field model that integrates spatial attention, temporal attention, and spatiotemporal propagation, enabling joint modeling of spatial and temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed GiFlow outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in spatiotemporal imputation. The code is available at https://github.com/zepengzhang/GiFlow.

</details>


### [204] [Towards Serverless Semi-Decentralized Federated Learning with Heterogeneous Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06687)
*Su Wang,Mung Chiang,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSD-FL，一种无需持久服务器的半去中心化联邦学习框架，通过轻量级D2D初始化实现集群形成，并利用有效损失函数与Cheeger不等式优化集群迭代，在异构网络和优化器环境下提升收敛速度与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化FL缺乏对集群形成机制的系统研究，且面临网络图结构、本地数据异构性与异构ML优化器三者复杂耦合的挑战，亟需一种无需服务器基础设施的集群优化方法。

Method: 提出SSD-FL框架：(1)轻量级一次性D2D初始化实现初始集群划分；(2)将全局轮次分割为簇内/簇间两个阶段；(3)设计集成设备特定优化器与网络图正则化的'有效损失函数'；(4)利用Cheeger不等式与共识间隙推导迭代聚类算法；(5)提出评分指标量化数据与优化器异构性并进行收敛/共识边界分析。

Result: 实验表明，SSD-FL在多种网络图结构、数据集和异构优化器场景下，相比三类去中心化FL基线方法，在收敛速度和通信效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: SSD-FL有效解决了去中心化FL中集群形成的优化难题，为无服务器异构联邦学习提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate cluster formation, involving the number and composition of clusters, in decentralized federated learning (FL) with heterogeneous machine learning (ML) optimizers. While clustering in centralized FL has enabled scalability and resource savings, its value and development in fully decentralized environments have yet to be explored. Optimizing cluster formation in such environments is challenging, especially due to the complex coupling between network graph structures, local data heterogeneity, and different local ML model optimizers. To address these challenges, we propose serverless semi-decentralized FL (SSD-FL), a methodology requiring no persistent server infrastructure. In SSD-FL, cluster formation occurs via a lightweight, one-time device-to-device (D2D) initialization phase, after which actual ML model training (alongside consensus and convergence processes) is fully serverless. Functionally, SSD-FL segments global rounds into intra-cluster and inter-cluster regimes, ensuring global convergence and consensus through novel "effective loss functions" that integrate device-specific ML optimizers with network graph-based regularization. Next, SSD-FL leverages the consensus gap via the Cheeger inequality to develop an iterative clustering algorithm evaluated against our derived convergence and consensus bounds, which incorporate a unique scoring metric to quantify data and optimizer heterogeneity across devices. Finally, experimental evaluation against three categories of decentralized FL methodologies validate that SSD-FL improves both convergence speeds and communication efficiency across various network graphs, datasets, and local optimizer regimes.

</details>


### [205] [The Geography of Algorithmic Judgment: LLM Intermediaries, Place Identity, and Racial Steering in Housing Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06694)
*Hana Samad,Trung Lam,Christoph Mügge-Durum,Michael Akinwumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过行为审计发现，LLM在住房推荐中的种族引导并非静态属性，而是模型解释权的涌现行为，受用户身份、偏好表达和城市空间逻辑共同影响，且不同城市的结果不可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM作为住房搜索中介时，是否会基于用户种族身份产生不同的空间推荐（种族引导），填补现有研究对此类新兴中介行为的理解空白。

Method: 对7个开源和闭源LLM进行行为审计，覆盖4个美国城市，采用公平住房配对测试方法，设置三个迭代提示条件（基线、加入生活方式偏好、偏好+身份条件）测试位置推荐。

Result: 种族引导是模型解释权的涌现行为而非静态属性；偏好条件测试增加了引导行为数量或重构了引导模式；不同城市结果差异显著，无法泛化。

Conclusion: LLM可能根据用户种族身份对相同住房偏好做出不同解读；城市不是中性测试单元；需要本地和领域专业知识确保公平住房承诺不被损害。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly assuming an intermediary role in housing search through the integration of listing platforms within conversational interfaces, mediating access to information, search, and recommendations within urban settings. We expand on prior work on racial steering in LLMs by conducting a behavioral audit of seven open-weight and closed-source LLMs across four U.S. cities, testing location recommendations across three iterative prompting conditions that progressively add lifestyle preference context and reflect fair housing paired-testing methodologies. We find that steering is an emergent behavior of the model's interpretive license rather than primarily a static property. Steering results from the interaction of a user's identity, preference articulation, and the spatial logic that a model has internalized about learned representations of place, preference, and opportunity in a given city, and how different types of users relate to it. While steering was present, it was not uniform in direction or magnitude across evaluated conditions. Preference-conditioned testing often increased or reconfigured the number of models that exhibited steering behaviors relative to baseline conditions, suggesting that LLMs may interpret what the same housing preference means differently depending on the racial identity of the user. Our findings also demonstrate that the city is not a neutral testing unit for LLM evaluation in place-based sectors, and results from one local market cannot be assumed to generalize to another. Local and domain expertise will be required in the housing sector to ensure that legal and institutional commitments to fair housing are not undermined while adopting AI tools that mediate spatial access.

</details>


### [206] [RECAP: Regression Evaluation for Continual Adaptation of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06698)
*Harsh Deshpande,Kushal Chawla,Sangwoo Cho,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAP是一个衡量在严格主动适应-测试协议下（仅在约束规范后泛化）持续学习现象（遗忘、回归、正向迁移）的基准测试。评估发现六种方法在四个LLM和三个变约束调度下性能无显著提升，且延迟更高，表明现有离线或反应式方法不适用于主动适应范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试假设静态约束集或带评估反馈的反应式协议，但生产环境中智能体系统面临不断变化的约束（如策略更新、合规阈值变化），且必须在下次交互时立即适应，几乎没有容错空间。这种主动适应场景在部署中常见却缺乏评测基准。

Method: 提出RECAP基准，采用严格主动适应-测试协议：提示优化方法仅接收约束规范，必须在看到任何测试数据前泛化。通过四个LLM（LLaMA-2-13B/70B、Mistral-7B、Mixtral-8x7B）和三种变约束调度，评估六种提示优化方法（包括APE、Opro、TextGrad、RV、InstOpt、DPO）的持续学习能力（遗忘、回归、正向迁移）。

Result: 六种方法在四种LLM和三种变约束调度下均未显示性能显著提升，即使牺牲更高延迟也没有改善。现有设计用于离线或反应式场景的方法无法胜任主动适应范式。

Conclusion: 强调亟需设计主动提示适应方法，使模型在部署中面对不断变化的需求保持鲁棒性。现有方法不足以应对生产环境的主动适应挑战。

Abstract: Production agentic systems routinely face evolving constraints and must comply from the very next interaction. Scenarios like a tool-call notification changing a compliance threshold or a policy update adding disclosure requirements fit this criteria, having close to no room for errors in production. This proactive adaptation setting is common in deployment, but absent from current benchmarks, which assume either static constraint sets or reactive protocols with evaluation feedback. We introduce RECAP, a benchmark that measures continual-learning phenomena (forgetting, regression, forward transfer) at the constraint level under a strictly proactive adapt-then-test protocol: prompt optimization methods receive only the constraint specification and must generalize before seeing any test data. Evaluating six methods across four LLMs and three schedules with evolving constraints, we find that these methods show no significant improvement in performance, even after incurring a higher latency. These methods, designed for offline or reactive settings, are inadequate for the proactive paradigm. Our work emphasizes the growing need for designing proactive prompt adaptation methods, where the models must remain robust to evolving needs in deployment.

</details>


### [207] [ShallowBench: Benchmarking Generative Drug Design Models on Shallow-Pocket Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06717)
*Saket Reddy,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ShallowBench基准，包含5,780个浅口袋靶点，用于评估生成式AI模型在低口袋性靶点（如KRAS和MYC）上的药物设计能力。现有模型在这些低凹度界面上表现不佳，需要新的架构或损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结构的药物生成模型主要针对深口袋靶点，对历史上‘不可成药’的浅口袋靶点（如KRAS、MYC）采样能力不足，缺乏专门评估此类挑战性靶点的严格基准。

Method: 从CrossDocked2020中严格筛选5,780个浅口袋靶点；通过计算Alpha Shape‘盖子’体积与底层蛋白质原子体素体积的差值，分离出低凹度但表面积足够的结合界面。

Result: 多项最先进的生成模型在这些低凹度界面上预测的结合亲和力较弱，表明现有方法在浅口袋靶点上存在系统性不足。

Conclusion: ShallowBench为生成式生物学模型提供了严格的基准测试，揭示了现有方法处理低口袋性靶点的局限性，强调了开发新架构或损失函数的必要性。

Abstract: While generative AI models have demonstrated remarkable success in structure-based drug design, they predominantly rely on deep binding pockets and struggle to sample effective ligands for challenging low-pocketability targets, such as the historically "undruggable" oncology targets KRAS and MYC. To address this gap, we introduce ShallowBench, a strictly curated benchmark of 5,780 shallow-pocket targets extracted from CrossDocked2020. By computing the difference between an Alpha Shape "lid" volume and the underlying protein atom voxel volume, we successfully isolated targets with low concavity while ensuring sufficient surface area for binding. Evaluating various state-of-the-art generative models reveals weaker predicted binding affinity on these low-concavity interfaces. ShallowBench therefore provides a rigorous benchmark for generative biology models and highlights the necessity of new architectural innovations or loss functions capable of navigating these challenging targets.

</details>


### [208] [MSAIC-Net: A Multi-Scale Attention and Imbalance-Aware Contrastive Network for ECG-Based Myocardial Substrate Abnormality Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06718)
*Canyu Lei,Fenglin Zhang,Derek Bivona,Cristiane Singulane,Jonathan Pan,Kenneth Bilchick,Amit R. Patel,Jianxin Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多尺度注意力增强卷积网络（MSAIC-Net），结合并行空洞卷积分支、通道注意力机制、不平衡感知监督对比学习和导联置换重要性分析，实现基于心电图的心肌基质异常（心肌瘢痕和心肌梗死）的准确、可解释检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电图（ECG）是检测心肌基质异常（如心肌瘢痕和心肌梗死）的低成本广泛可用工具，但基于ECG的检测面临导联依赖性异质性、高维多导联信号、类别不平衡以及深度学习模型可解释性有限等挑战。

Method: 提出MSAIC-Net：采用并行空洞卷积分支提取多尺度时间感受野的ECG特征；利用通道注意力机制自适应重加权导联和特征通道表示；引入不平衡感知监督对比学习策略处理类别不平衡并增强特征可分性；采用导联置换重要性量化各导联贡献以提升可解释性。

Result: 在UVA低数据队列（心肌瘢痕分类）和PTB-XL公共数据集（心肌梗死识别）上，MSAIC-Net优于基线模型，尤其在低数据UVA队列上改进更为显著。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于ECG的心肌基质异常检测提供了一种有效且可解释的方法。

Abstract: Myocardial substrate abnormalities, such as myocardial scar and myocardial infarction (MI), are associated with adverse cardiovascular outcomes. Electrocardiography (ECG) provides a low-cost and widely available tool for detecting these abnormalities, but ECG-based detection remains challenging due to heterogeneous lead-dependent manifestations, high-dimensional multi-lead signals, class imbalance, and the limited interpretability of deep learning models. We propose a multi-scale attention-enhanced convolutional network (MSAIC-Net) for ECG-based myocardial substrate abnormality detection. MSAIC-Net employs parallel atrous convolutional branches to extract ECG features across multiple temporal receptive fields. %, enabling the model to capture both local and longer-range temporal patterns. Channel attention is then used to adaptively reweight informative lead-wise and feature-channel representations. To address class imbalance and improve feature separability, we introduce a novel imbalance-aware supervised contrastive learning strategy that encourages samples from the same class to form compact representations while increasing separation between abnormal and normal samples. Lead-wise permutation importance is further incorporated to quantify the contribution of each ECG lead and improve model interpretability. The proposed method was evaluated on two complementary datasets: a low-data institutional cohort from the University of Virginia (UVA) Health System for myocardial scar classification and the large-scale public PTB-XL dataset from PhysioNet for MI identification. Experimental results show that MSAIC-Net outperforms baseline models, with particularly pronounced improvements in the low-data UVA cohort. Overall, the proposed framework provides an effective and interpretable approach for ECG-based detection of myocardial substrate abnormalities.

</details>


### [209] [Flatland: The Adventures of Gradient Descent with Large Step Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06722)
*Leonardo Galli,Curtis Fox,Wiebke Bartolomaeus,Mark Schmidt,Holger Rauhut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的"大步长"定义，仅需梯度满足局部Lipschitz（甚至Hölder）连续性，并设计了自适应一阶方法，使梯度下降从训练一开始就运行在稳定性边缘（EoS），步长与锐度的乘积始终高于阈值2，同时能最小化锐度至全局最小值。研究发现过早进入全局平坦区域会降低收敛速度和泛化能力，而通过自稳定化机制让GD进入稍尖锐的谷底可将失败训练转化为成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练中目标函数通常不是全局L-光滑的，使得确定保证梯度下降收敛的最大步长这一经典问题长期未解。现有理论难以应对非全局光滑情形下的大步长选择。

Method: 1. 提出仅需局部Lipschitz（或Hölder）连续性的大步长统一定义；2. 设计自适应一阶方法，确保步长从训练开始就处于稳定性边缘；3. 利用自稳定化论证，允许梯度下降进入稍尖锐的谷底。

Result: 1. 损失非单调下降，步长与锐度乘积始终高于EoS阈值2；2. 能够将锐度最小化至全局最小值；3. 过早进入全局平坦区域反而降低收敛速度和泛化能力；4. 自稳定化机制可将失败训练转化为成功。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实践上回答了非全局L-光滑目标函数下梯度下降最大步长的问题，揭示了EoS现象的本质，并提供了实用的自适应优化方法。同时挑战了"平坦极小值更好"的传统认知，指出训练初期过早进入平坦区域反而不利。

Abstract: The training of neural networks often entails objective functions that are not globally $L$-smooth. For these functions, it is both theoretically and practically difficult to reply to the question: what is the largest possible step size that ensures the convergence of gradient descent (GD)? We address this longstanding open question in deep learning by providing a unifying definition of "large" step sizes that requires only local Lipschitz (or even Hölder) continuity of the gradient. We design first-order adaptive methods that provably yield large step sizes and show that they operate at the edge of stability (EoS) right from the start of the training. In particular, the loss decreases nonmonotonically and the product between the step size and sharpness, i.e., the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian, stays above the EoS threshold of 2 throughout training. Using our method, we are also able to minimize the sharpness all the way down to its global minimum. Contrary to expectation, we find that encountering globally-flat regions too early in the training may both slow down convergence and jeopardize the generalization ability of the network. Exploiting a self-stabilization argument, we allow GD to enter slightly sharper valleys and turn unsuccessful training runs into very successful ones.

</details>


### [210] [Synthics: Synthetic Physics-like Datasets for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06724)
*Jari Vepsäläinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法从方程语料库中生成结构相似的合成回归数据集的方法，通过非侵入式探测定义物理有效域并模拟真实实验条件采样，生成的方程在8个结构特征上均匹配费曼语料库，在下游超参数调优任务中表现与真实数据调优相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据收集困难且成本高，合成数据生成需要忠实反映真实观测数据的结构，现有方法在结构保真度上存在不足。

Method: 使用贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法（Bayesian PCFG）捕捉方程语料库的代数结构并采样新方程；通过非侵入式探测表征每个方程的适用域及变量间约束；采用混合均匀分布与截断正态分布从有效域的子范围采样输入，模拟真实实验条件。

Result: 生成的方程在全部8个结构特征上与费曼方程语料库匹配（无平滑的纯概率文法仅匹配2个）；在下游任务中，基于合成数据调优的梯度提升回归器在20个配置中平均选择第6优配置，与真实数据调优结果相同，显著优于随机表达式树（第10）和噪声（第19）。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯先验对于小规模语料库的结构保真度至关重要，该方法能生成结构上忠实于原始方程且物理上合理的合成数据，在机器学习下游任务中表现与真实数据相当。

Abstract: Representative data is fundamental in machine learning, as limited data hinders generalisation. Collecting sufficient real-world samples is often infeasible. Synthetic data generation offers a practical solution, but only if the generated data faithfully reflects the structure of real observations. In this paper, a method for generating synthetic regression datasets that structurally resemble physics equations from a given equation corpus is presented. The approach uses a Bayesian Probabilistic Context-Free Grammar to capture the underlying algebraic structure of the corpus, from which novel equations are sampled. To ensure the generated inputs lie within a physically meaningful domain, the applicability domain is characterised for each equation through non-intrusive probing, also recovering inter-variable constraints. Input sampling further mimics realistic experimental conditions by drawing from random sub-ranges of the valid domain with mixed uniform and truncated normal distributions. The generated data is statistically validated against the Feynman equation corpus using Kolmogorov-Smirnov tests. The generated equations match the corpus on all of the eight studied structural features, compared to only two for an unsmoothed purely probabilistic grammar, demonstrating that the Bayesian prior is essential for structural fidelity given the size of the corpus. In a downstream hyperparameter-tuning task, a gradient-boosted regressor tuned on the synthetic data picks, on average, the 6th-best configuration out of 20 on real data, matching the result of tuning on real data itself and substantially outperforming random expression trees (10th) and noise (19th).

</details>


### [211] [TorchKM: A GPU-Oriented Library for Kernel Learning and Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06742)
*Yikai Zhang,Gaoxiang Jia,Jie Ding,Boxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TorchKM是一个面向核机器（如SVM、核逻辑回归、核分位数回归）的开源库，支持GPU加速，通过智能复用矩阵运算加速完整训练与模型选择流程，提供scikit-learn风格API，在保持竞争力的预测性能的同时显著加速，可通过PyPI安装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核机器方法在大规模数据上的训练与模型选择效率较低，现有实现缺乏对GPU友好的线性代数优化，且难以高效复用计算中间结果，导致整体流程耗时较长。

Method: 设计scikit-learn风格API的GPU加速核机器库，通过智能复用矩阵运算（如核矩阵计算、分解等）来加速完整训练与模型选择（如交叉验证、超参数搜索）全过程。

Result: 基准测试显示，TorchKM在预测性能上与标准基线相当，同时取得了显著的计算加速效果，且代码已在GitHub开源并支持PyPI安装。

Conclusion: TorchKM为核机器提供了高效、易用的GPU加速实现，通过矩阵运算复用在保持预测能力的同时大幅提升训练与模型选择效率，有助于推动核方法在大规模实际问题中的应用。

Abstract: TorchKM is an open-source library for kernel machines, including support vector machines, kernel logistic regression, and kernel quantile regression, with GPU acceleration. The library features a scikit-learn-style API and is designed to exploit GPU-friendly linear algebra, accelerating the full training and model-selection pipeline through intelligent reuse of matrix operations. Benchmarks show competitive predictive performance together with substantial speedups over standard baselines. Code and documentation are available at https://github.com/YikaiZhang95/torchkm, and the package can be easily installed via PyPI.

</details>


### [212] [Learn to Match: Two-Sided Matching with Temporally Extended Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06744)
*Haijing Zong,Yancheng Liang,Boyang Zhou,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Learn2Match，一个基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的动态双边匹配市场基准平台。该框架将双边匹配建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈，支持面试筛选、匹配决策和关系解除等时序决策，并引入信息摩擦损失衡量信息不完全揭示导致的福利缺口。实验表明，独立PPO在累积社会福利和后悔方面优于bandit基线，但在信息摩擦损失上仍较高，说明端到端MARL尚缺乏匹配bandit方法的协调探索结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有匹配模型通常将信息交互过程简化为即时亚高斯反馈，忽略了支付相关信息逐步揭示并影响未来匹配决策的现实场景（如面试、重复互动、学习与分离等动态过程）。作者旨在填补这一空白，为动态匹配市场提供更贴近现实的建模框架和基准工具。

Method: 将动态双边匹配建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈（POMG），包含：昂贵的匹配前筛选、含噪的匹配后观测、演化的潜在特征以及内生性的关系延续或解除。在此基础上构建Learn2Match平台，支持智能体在以下三个方面做分散决策：（1）面试谁；（2）与谁匹配；（3）何时解除匹配。评估指标包括：后悔值、社会福利以及信息摩擦损失（衡量因潜在偏好不完全揭示导致的福利缺口）。

Result: 独立PPO在累积社会福利和累积后悔上优于bandit风格的CA-ETC基线，但PPO的信息摩擦损失更高，说明端到端MARL在协调探索方面仍不及匹配bandit方法的结构化探索能力。

Conclusion: Learn2Match可作为下一代匹配市场算法的基准：需要兼具RL的适应性、bandit算法的统计严谨性，以及稳定匹配机制的结构规范性。当前MARL方法在协调探索上仍需改进。

Abstract: Two-sided matching markets often involve information that unfolds over time through interviews, repeated interaction, learning, and separation. Existing matching models typically reduce this process to immediate sub-Gaussian feedback about fixed preferences, missing settings where payoff-relevant information is revealed gradually and changes future matching decisions. We introduce a framework with temporally extended feedback, that formulates two-sided matching as a partially observable Markov game with costly pre-match screening, noisy post-match observations, evolving latent profiles, and endogenous continuation or dissolution. We instantiate this framework in Learn2Match, a multi-agent reinforcement-learning benchmark for dynamic matching markets. Learn2Match supports decentralized decision making over whom to interview, whom to match with, and when to dissolve a match, while evaluating policies using regret, social welfare, and an information-friction loss that measures the welfare gap caused by incomplete revelation of latent preferences. We find that independent PPO achieves higher cumulative social welfare and lower cumulative regret than the bandit-style CA-ETC baseline under temporally extended feedback, demonstrating the promise of MARL for dynamic matching markets. However, PPO still incurs higher information-friction loss, revealing that end-to-end MARL does not yet provide the coordinated exploration structure of matching-bandit methods. These results position Learn2Match as a benchmark for developing the next generation of matching-market algorithms: methods that are adaptive like RL agents, statistically disciplined like bandit algorithms, and structurally aware like stable-matching mechanisms.

</details>


### [213] [Performance Variation in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06746)
*Haruto Tanaka,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出深度强化学习算法在独立运行间存在性能变异性的问题，并提出了基于百分位数的统计量和可视化方法（min-max IPR和逐运行百分位高亮）来更准确地评估运行间变异，通过三个案例验证了该方法能有效揭示不同算法（如PPO、SAC、TD-MPC等）的性能变异特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习算法经常面临运行间鲁棒性低的问题，即同一算法多次运行的性能差异很大。然而现有研究通常只报告平均性能的不确定性，缺乏专门评估运行间变异的方法，导致问题被低估或误解。

Method: 提出了基于百分位数的统计指标（min-max IPR）和可视化方法（逐运行百分位高亮），利用样本百分位数的标准性质来量化并展示运行间的性能变异。

Result: 通过三个案例研究验证：1) LayerNorm和倒数第二层归一化缩小了PPO的性能变异，但对SAC影响不大；2) TD-MPC在四种算法中变异最小且数据效率最高；3) DQN和Rainbow在五个Atari环境中显示出相似水平的性能变异。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于百分位数的工具易于解释，能提供关于运行间性能变异的丰富信息，弥补了传统不确定性估计的不足，有助于更全面地评估强化学习算法的稳定性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms often suffer from low run-to-run robustness, manifesting as significant performance variation across independent runs of identically configured agents. Although this issue poses a spectrum of challenges across research and practice, relatively few studies develop methods to evaluate it; RL research instead often reports uncertainty in the estimated mean performance. In this paper, we outline the limitations of conventional uncertainty and variation estimates, particularly their misalignment with purpose and the risk of underreporting. We then propose an alternative percentile-based statistic and visualization method, min-max IPR and run-wise percentile highlighting, respectively. These percentile-based tools are easy to interpret and rely on standard properties of sample percentiles, providing rich information about run-to-run performance variation. We demonstrate this through three case studies. First, we show that LayerNorm and penultimate-layer normalizations narrow performance variation in PPO, whereas the variation is mostly unchanged in SAC. Second, we compare PPO, SAC, TD-MPC, and TD-MPC2, and show TD-MPC exhibits the least variation while being the most data efficient among the four. Finally, in a comparison of DQN and Rainbow on five Atari environments, we show that both algorithms exhibit similar levels of performance variation.

</details>


### [214] [A Rolling-Window Framework for Churn Prediction and Behavioral Driver Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06776)
*Muhammad Jawad Mufti,Omar Hammad,Haitham Saleh,Muqaddas Gull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个时间显式的流失预测框架，通过滚动行为窗口在非合同按需服务环境中实现持续、可解释的流失风险评估。基于30天观察窗口和30天评估窗口的时序设计，结合特征与序列学习方法，在真实大规模数据集上取得87.6%准确率、0.94 AUC（特征模型）和96.1%召回率（序列模型），并在未来未见数据上保持83%+准确率和0.91+AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流失预测方法通常依赖简化时间假设或单点行为表征，难以支持持续风险评估、可解释性及真实部署需求，尤其是在非合同按需服务场景中客户流失并非显式观察而需从行为不活跃推断。

Method: 提出时间显式流失预测框架，采用30天滚动行为窗口（观察期）与30天未来流失评估窗口的严格时序分离设计；整合基于特征（如RF、XGBoost等）与基于序列（如LSTM）的学习方法，在统一时间框架下进行实例级流失风险估计。

Result: 特征模型准确率87.6%、ROC-AUC 0.94；序列模型召回率高达96.1%，能有效捕捉时间维度的流失模式。在未来未见数据上，准确率仍保持83%以上、ROC-AUC超0.91，无需模型重训练即展现良好的时间偏移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精心设计的时序框架比模型复杂度本身对实现稳健、可解释且易于部署的流失预测更为关键，为动态服务环境中的流失导向决策支持提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction is a central task in customer analytics, particularly in non-contractual, pay-per-use service environments where disengagement is not explicitly observed and must be inferred from behavioral inactivity. Existing churn prediction approaches often rely on simplified temporal assumptions or single-point representations of customer behavior, which limit their ability to support continuous risk assessment, interpretability, and realistic deployment over time. This study proposes a temporally explicit churn prediction framework that models customer behavior using rolling behavioral windows, enabling repeated and instance-level churn risk estimation as customer activity evolves. Customer behavior is summarized within a fixed 30-day observation window, followed by a 30-day future churn evaluation window, ensuring a clear temporal separation between behavioral evidence and churn outcomes. The framework integrates feature-based and sequence-based learning approaches within a unified temporal design. The proposed approach is evaluated on a large-scale, real-world dataset from a non-contractual service platform. Empirical results demonstrate strong and stable predictive performance, with accuracy reaching 87.6% and ROC-AUC of 0.94 for the feature-based model, while the sequence-based model achieves recall as high as 96.1% by capturing temporal disengagement patterns. Evaluation on future unseen data confirms meaningful robustness under temporal shift, with accuracy remaining above 83% and ROC-AUC exceeding 0.91 without model retraining. Overall, the findings highlight that carefully designed temporal framing, rather than model complexity alone, is critical for achieving robust, interpretable, and deployment-ready churn prediction. The study provides a practical foundation for churn-oriented decision support in dynamic service environments.

</details>


### [215] [Federated Foundation Models over Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06786)
*Kasra Borazjani,Fardis Nadimi,Payam Abdisarabshali,Owen Palinski,Allan Salihovic,Dinh Nguyen,Minghui Liwang,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态多任务联邦基础模型（M3T FedFMs）集成到车辆网络中的前瞻性愿景，旨在统一多模态多任务基础模型的表达能力与联邦学习的隐私保护分布式学习能力。文章介绍了M3T FedFMs的训练/微调原理，讨论了其在车辆网络中的代表性用例，识别了车辆环境中的关键部署约束，并提出了前瞻性研究方向。通过在Waymo开放数据集上的案例研究，展示了M3T FedFMs在车辆网络中的潜力，并开源了实现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态多任务基础模型（M3T FMs）与联邦学习（FL）的结合在车辆网络领域尚处于未充分探索阶段，作者希望将M3T FMs的强大多模态表达能力与FL的隐私保护和分布式学习优势相融合，以推动下一代车辆智能的发展。

Method: 首先介绍M3T FedFMs的基础训练/微调原理；然后讨论其在车辆网络中的代表性用例；识别车辆环境中的关键约束挑战；提出前瞻性研究方向；最后通过Waymo开放数据集进行案例研究验证。

Result: 通过Waymo开放数据集上的案例研究，展示了M3T FedFMs在车辆网络中的潜力，并公开发布了实现代码以促进可重复性和后续研究。

Conclusion: M3T FedFMs有望统一多模态基础模型的表达能力与联邦学习的隐私保护能力，为下一代车辆智能提供重要支撑，但仍需克服车辆环境中的诸多部署挑战，未来研究空间广阔。

Abstract: This paper presents a forward-looking vision for integrating the emerging multi-modal multi-task federated foundation models (M3T FedFMs) into vehicular networks, with the goal of unifying the expressive power of multi-modal multi-task foundation models (M3T FMs) with the privacy-preserving and distributed learning capabilities of federated learning (FL). Given the largely underexplored nature of this research direction, we first introduce the fundamental training/fine-tuning principles of M3T FedFMs. We then discuss a range of their representative use cases in vehicular networks, illustrating the significant potential of M3T FedFMs to enable next-generation vehicular intelligence. Afterwards, we identify key constraints inherent to vehicular environments that challenge the practical deployment of M3T FedFMs, and articulate a set of forward-looking research directions to address these challenges. Furthermore, through a case study conducted on a real-world vehicular dataset (i.e., Waymo Open Dataset), we demonstrate the promise of M3T FedFMs for vehicular networks and release our implementation to facilitate reproducibility and stimulate research in this emerging area (repository: https://github.com/KasraBorazjani/vehicular-fedfm)

</details>


### [216] [Interpreting Learning Under Competing Models: Joint and Stepwise Approaches for Dynamic Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06804)
*Yawen Ma,Sahoko Ishida,Kate Cain,Gabriel Wallin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在数字学习环境中，当项目-技能结构（Q矩阵）未知时，联合估计与分步估计两种分析方法对学习结论的影响。通过动态认知诊断模型分析阅读游戏数据，发现联合分析更可靠，尤其在项目技能结构不确定且项目池随年级变化时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字学习环境记录学习者对单个项目的反应，可以研究特定技能的发展。当每个项目测量的技能（Q矩阵）未知时，分析师需要决定是联合估计Q矩阵与学习过程，还是先确定Q矩阵再研究学习。这一决策可能改变对学习者发展过程的实质性结论。

Method: 使用动态认知诊断模型，分析从二年级到三年级的两个阅读游戏数据，测量词汇和理解能力。利用项目文本嵌入提供Q矩阵的先验信息。比较联合分析与偏差校正的分步分析两种方法，并通过模拟研究识别两种分析产生分歧的条件。

Result: 联合分析和偏差校正的分步分析都认为大多数学习者走向掌握两种技能，但在三年级时仍只有部分掌握技能的学习者数量上存在分歧，这将改变阅读进展的报告方式。模拟研究表明，当项目-技能结构不确定且项目池在不同年级间变化时，联合分析更可靠。

Conclusion: 分析方法的决策（联合vs分步）会改变关于学习者发展的实质性结论。在项目技能结构不确定且项目池变化的情况下，联合分析更为可靠。研究提供了两种分析的R代码。

Abstract: Digital learning environments record learners' responses to individual items, making it possible to study the development of specific skills rather than overall scores. Drawing conclusions about learning from these data requires a model that links responses to latent skills and tracks how mastery changes over time. When the skills measured by each item are unknown, the analyst must decide whether to estimate this structure, the Q-matrix, jointly with the learning process, or to establish it first and study learning afterwards. We show that this decision can change substantive conclusions about how learners develop. Using dynamic cognitive diagnostic models, we analyse data from two reading games measuring vocabulary and comprehension from Grade 2 to Grade 3, with item-text embeddings providing prior information for the unknown Q-matrix. A joint analysis and a bias-corrected stepwise analysis agree that most learners move toward mastering both skills, but disagree about how many remain only partially proficient at Grade 3, changing how reading progress would be reported. A simulation study identifies when the two analyses diverge and shows that joint analysis is more reliable when the item-skill structure is uncertain and the item pool changes between grades. We provide R code for both analyses.

</details>


### [217] [SCALE: Scalable Cross-Attention Learning with Extrapolation for Agentic Workflow Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06820)
*Zhifei Xu,Jierui Lan,Zixuan Liang,Aiji Liang,Jinxi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALE是一个基于深度强化学习的调度器，通过交叉注意力指针网络和结构化表示正则化（SRR），能够在无需微调的情况下泛化到未见过的集群规模，在扩展到48节点时比无SRR的架构平均响应时间降低8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度强化学习的调度器与固定集群规模绑定，当服务器数量发生变化时需要重新训练，缺乏对集群规模变化的泛化能力。

Method: 提出SCALE调度器，采用交叉注意力指针网络（任务特征查询服务器特征），使架构天然支持任意数量服务器；并引入结构化表示正则化（SRR），包含去相关损失和向标准正态分布的KL惩罚，以稳定不同输入规模下的特征统计量。

Result: 在16节点上训练，直接测试于32和48节点，SCALE在N=48时相比无SRR的相同架构将平均响应时间降低了8.9%。

Conclusion: 显式正则化（SRR）对于缩小规模泛化差距是必要的，SCALE实现了无需微调即可泛化到未见过的集群规模。

Abstract: Agentic Large Language Model (LLM) systems decompose complex tasks into workflow Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) whose primitives must be scheduled on heterogeneous clusters. Existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) schedulers are tied to a fixed cluster size and require retraining whenever the number of servers changes. We propose SCALE (Scalable Cross-Attention Learning with Extrapolation), a DRL scheduler that generalizes to unseen cluster scales without fine-tuning. SCALE employs a cross-attention pointer network where task features query against server features, so the architecture accepts any number of servers by construction. We observe, however, that permutation-invariant architecture alone does not guarantee good performance at new scales - the attention feature undergoes distribution shift as the server count grows. To counter this, we introduce Structured Representation Regularization (SRR): a decorrelation loss combined with a KL penalty toward the standard normal, which keeps feature statistics stable regardless of input size. Trained on 16 nodes and tested directly on 32 and 48 nodes, SCALE reduces average response time by 8.9% at N=48 relative to the same architecture without SRR, confirming that explicit regularization is necessary to close the scale-generalization gap.

</details>


### [218] [PandaAI: A Practical Agent CQ2 for Neuro-symbolic Data Analysis And Integrated Decision-Making in Quantitative Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06823)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PandaAI是一个面向金融时间序列决策的闭环神经符号LLM智能体,通过市场机制建模和受限alpha生成,在沪深300数据上实现了18.2%的Rank IC提升和25.7%的最大回撤降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在金融时序决策中面临信噪比低和非平稳性挑战,而LLM的推理能力尚未被有效利用以解决这些问题。

Method: 提出PandaAI,包含领域微调LLM、市场机制建模、受限alpha生成以及闭环神经符号架构,通过双通道自适应方法实现风险感知的序列决策。

Result: 在CSI 300股票数据上,相比最先进的时序模型,Rank IC提升18.2%,最大回撤降低25.7%。

Conclusion: PandaAI通过受限LLM生成和双通道自适应方法,为LLM在高风险序列决策场景中的部署提供了通用范式。

Abstract: While deep learning has excelled in various domains, its application to sequential decision-making in finance remains challenging due to the low Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) and non-stationarity of financial data. Leveraging the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose \textbf{PandaAI}, a closed-loop neuro-symbolic LLM agent with market regime modeling and constrained alpha generation, which bridges general LLM reasoning with financial rigor and suppresses the financial toxicity of LLM-generated outputs. To bridge the gap between general linguistic capability and financial rigor, we fine-tune a domain-specific LLM. Furthermore, we integrate this LLM into a modular architecture and form a closed-loop system. Unlike traditional models that optimize isolated prediction metrics, \textbf{PandaAI} is designed as a neuro-symbolic agent that navigates the complex, real-world financial environment with explicit risk awareness. Extensive experiments on CSI 300 stock data show that \textbf{PandaAI} achieves a $18.2\%$ higher Rank IC and $25.7\%$ lower maximum drawdown than state-of-the-art time-series models. Our constrained LLM generation and dual-channel adaptation method provide a general paradigm for LLM deployment in high-stakes sequential decision-making scenarios.

</details>


### [219] [Architecture Shapes Transfer Specificity in Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06827)
*D Yang Eng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验和多个PDE基准测试，系统研究了隐式神经表征（INR）中的迁移学习现象，发现迁移幅度和迁移特异性是可分离的：SIREN倾向于广泛复用权重，而ReLU MLP和傅里叶特征MLP更具源选择性。研究建议在科学机器学习中评估架构选择时应使用显式控制条件，而非仅看迁移幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在坐标网络中，warm-start增益常被用作迁移学习的度量指标，但该增益究竟是反映了源任务特有的结构信息，还是仅仅是通用权重复用（generic weight reuse）所致，这一核心问题尚不明确。需要设计控制实验来区分这两种情况。

Method: 研究了三种INR家族（SIREN、ReLU MLP、傅里叶特征MLP），采用三类测试：(1)受控解析测试（含独立种子随机对照）；(2)2D lid-driven-cavity Navier-Stokes基准；(3)1D PDE参考解套件（热方程、粘性Burgers方程、聚焦三次NLS方程）。通过比较真实迁移与随机对照迁移的差异来量化迁移特异性。

Result: (1) 在10种子受控1D几何测试中，傅里叶特征结构化迁移最大(33.1×)，其次是SIREN(23.0×)和ReLU(10.7×)，但ReLU选择性更强（随机对照迁移仅0.41×，而SIREN为14.24×）。(2) 在受控双参数1D族测试中，ReLU在默认设置下给出最清晰的结构化与对照分离，傅里叶特征需调优带宽后才提升。(3) 在Navier-Stokes和更广泛1D PDE套件中，无单一架构在所有方程上占优，但模式一致：SIREN广泛复用权重，ReLU和傅里叶特征更具源选择性。(4) 静态诊断指标仍然较弱，启发式标度律A_transfer ∝ 1/Δt²被拒绝。

Conclusion: 迁移特异性（transfer specificity）可作为坐标网络的一个有用诊断指标；在科学机器学习中，架构选择应在显式控制条件下进行评估，而不能仅依据迁移幅度。建议使用随机对照等控制实验来区分真实的源结构迁移与通用的权重复用。

Abstract: Transfer in coordinate networks is often measured by warm-start gain, but whether that gain reflects source-specific structure or generic weight reuse is less clear. We study this question across three implicit neural representation (INR) families, SIREN, ReLU MLPs, and Fourier-feature MLPs, using controlled analytic tests, a 2D lid-driven-cavity Navier--Stokes benchmark, and 1D PDE reference-solution suites for heat, viscous Burgers, and focusing cubic NLS. The analytic tests use independent-seed random controls, while the PDE benchmarks use alternate same-family source controls and auxiliary ablations.
  Across settings, transfer magnitude and transfer specificity separate clearly. In a 10-seed controlled 1D geometric test, Fourier Features show the largest structured transfer ($33.1\times$), followed by SIREN ($23.0\times$) and ReLU ($10.7\times$), but ReLU is far more selective: random-control transfer is $0.41\times$ for ReLU versus $14.24\times$ for SIREN. On a controlled two-parameter 1D family, the ranking changes: ReLU gives the clearest structured-versus-control separation at default settings, whereas Fourier Features improve only after bandwidth retuning. In Navier--Stokes and the broader 1D PDE suite, no single architecture dominates every equation, yet the same pattern remains: SIREN often reuses weights broadly, whereas ReLU and, in some equations, Fourier Features are more source-selective. Static diagnostics remain weak, and the heuristic scaling law $A_{\text{transfer}} \propto 1/Δt^2$ is rejected in the implemented 1D audit.
  These results position transfer specificity as a useful diagnostic for coordinate networks and suggest that architecture selection in scientific machine learning should be evaluated under explicit control conditions, not by transfer magnitude alone.

</details>


### [220] [Hearing the Unspoken: Language Model Priors for Acoustic Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06833)
*Jiani Xie,Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“语义策略”（Semantic Gambit）的新型对抗攻击方法，通过利用大语言模型（LLM）提供实时预测上下文，突破了实时自动语音识别（ASR）系统因因果约束（仅在局部信息上做决策）而带来的信息瓶颈，将语料级词错误率（WER）提升至35.6%，是当前最先进方法的3倍。研究表明，常见的低延迟LLM工具可被系统性地利用来破坏实时ASR流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时ASR系统在严格时间约束下处理语音输入，其转录决策基于不完整信息（因果约束）。这种因果约束对攻击者构成了信息瓶颈，显著限制了攻击性能。作者旨在打破这一瓶颈，设计不受此因果限制的更强攻击方法。

Method: 提出Semantic Gambit攻击：通过将大语言模型（LLM）提供的实时预测上下文注入对抗扰动生成过程中，增强攻击者的能力，从而规避ASR系统因局部信息决策带来的防御优势。

Result: 实验表明，该攻击能将语料级词错误率（WER）提升至35.6%，相比当前最先进的方法实现了三倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了常见的低延迟LLM工具如何被利用来系统地破坏实时ASR管道，暴露了ASR系统在结合LLM增强的对抗攻击面前存在脆弱性。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems operating in real-time settings must process acoustic input under strict temporal constraints, where transcription decisions are inherently made on incomplete information. This causal constraint serves as an information bottleneck on attackers, significantly limiting attack performance. Our new Semantic Gambit attack breaks this causal limitation by augmenting the adversary with predictive context derived from a Large Language Model in real-time. Our experiments show that this form of augmentation can elevate the corpus-level Word Error Rate to 35.6% -- a three-fold increase over the current state-of-the-art. Ultimately, this work reveals how common, low-latency LLM tooling can be exploited to systematically subvert real-time ASR pipelines.

</details>


### [221] [The Geometry of Last-Layer Model Stealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06854)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何方法解释如何利用已有方法窃取机器学习模型，展示了完美复制Transformer网络最后一层的条件，揭示了深层网络的限制，并明确界定了模型可被窃取与不可被窃取的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究机器学习模型在面对已知攻击方法时的脆弱性，明确界定模型哪些部分可被逆向工程窃取、哪些部分不可被窃取，为模型安全防护提供理论边界。

Method: 采用几何分析方法，系统考察Transformer网络的层级结构，从最终层到隐藏层逐层分析逆向工程的可能性与条件。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下可完美复制Transformer网络的最后一层；但对于更深层的隐藏网络，仅通过观察最终输出无法实现完全的逆向工程。

Conclusion: 研究清晰地勾勒出模型可被窃取与不可被窃取的边界：最终层在特定条件下可被完美复制，而隐藏层存在明确的逆向工程限制，无法被完整还原。

Abstract: This paper uses geometry to explain how a machine learning model can be stolen using an already existing well-known method. The author has shown the exact conditions required to perfectly copy the final layer of a transformer network. When looking deeper into the hidden layers the author has explained clear limits. The author has also demonstrated that a hidden network cannot be fully reverse engineered just by looking at the final results. The research clearly maps out what can and cannot be stolen from a model.

</details>


### [222] [Modeling Nonlinear Feature Interactions with Product-Unit Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06861)
*Ziyuan Li,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PURe通过显式建模特征交互提升了预测精度、鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLP隐式处理非线性特征交互导致表征纠缠，影响鲁棒性和可解释性。

Method: 提出乘积单元残差网络(PURe)，集成乘性乘积单元与残差连接，显式建模交叉特征耦合并稳定优化。

Result: 在合成基准和真实数据集上，PURe在预测精度、对高斯噪声的鲁棒性、小样本效率以及基于SHAP的交互可解释性方面均优于或持平MLP基线。

Conclusion: PURe实现了竞争性或更优的性能，在低数据场景下具有更好的鲁棒性和样本效率，并增强了交互层面的可解释性。

Abstract: Understanding nonlinear feature interactions is crucial in science and engineering, yet standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) often capture such interactions only implicitly, leading to entangled representations that can impair robustness and interpretability. We investigate product-unit residual networks (PURe) that integrate multiplicative product units with residual connections to explicitly model cross-feature couplings while stabilizing optimization. We conduct a systematic evaluation on an interaction-driven synthetic benchmark and two real-world datasets, assessing predictive accuracy, robustness to Gaussian feature noise, and performance under limited training data, and we compare real- and complex-valued variants under a matched parameter budget. Beyond accuracy, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based interaction analyses show that PURe learns more concentrated and structurally coherent interaction patterns than MLP baselines. Overall, PURe achieves competitive or improved performance, better robustness and sample efficiency in low-data regimes, and enhanced interaction-level interpretability.

</details>


### [223] [Product units in gated recurrent units improve nuclear-mass prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06866)
*Ziyuan Li,Paulo S. A. Freitas,John W. Clark,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于复数域、融合乘性交互与乘积单元变换的门控循环单元（AM-PU-GRU）模型，在原子核质量预测任务上取得了显著优于现有最先进模型和实值GRU基线的性能，插值RMSE为0.227±0.004 MeV，外推RMSE为0.179±0.015 MeV，建立了基于序列的核质量预测新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用机器学习预测原子核质量可补充理论模型，推动对核图表中未知区域的探索，而现有的机器学习方法在捕捉长期依赖关系和幅度-相位联合动力学方面存在局限，亟需更强大的序列建模架构。

Method: 提出复数域下的加性-乘性乘积单元门控循环单元（AM-PU-GRU），在循环单元中集成乘性交互（multiplicative interactions）和乘积单元变换（product-unit transformations），在复数域中计算以同时捕获幅度与相位动力学信息。

Result: 在AME2016和AME2020原子质量评估数据上，AM-PU-GRU模型在插值任务中取得0.227±0.004 MeV的RMSE，在外推任务中取得0.179±0.015 MeV的RMSE，均优于其他最先进机器学习模型、实值GRU基线及乘积单元消融变体，且对不同的理论先验（WS4和SEMF）保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 复数域乘积单元循环网络为基于序列的核质量预测建立了新的性能基准，证明该架构在捕捉复杂核物理动态方面的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: The prediction of masses of atomic nuclei using machine learning can complement theoretical models and advance the exploration of poorly known domains of the nuclear chart. We propose a machine learning technique based on gated recurrent units (GRU), which have demonstrated competitive performance in nuclear-mass prediction by exploiting long-term dependencies. By integrating multiplicative interactions and product-unit transformations within recurrent units, we report significant improvements in nuclear-mass prediction. Computations are performed in the complex domain to jointly capture amplitude and phase dynamics. For interpolation and temporal-extrapolation tasks based on the atomic mass evaluation (AME2016 and AME2020), the complex additive-multiplicative product-unit gated recurrent unit (AM-PU-GRU) model consistently achieves the lowest prediction errors, with an interpolation RMSE of 0.227 $\pm$ 0.004 MeV and an extrapolation RMSE of 0.179 $\pm$ 0.015 MeV. These results surpass other state-of-the-art machine learning models and also outperform the real-valued GRU baseline and product-unit ablation variants, while remaining robust to different theoretical priors, including WS4 and SEMF. Our findings establish complex-valued product-unit recurrent networks as a new benchmark for sequence-based nuclear-mass prediction.

</details>


### [224] [Evidence-Grounded Ensemble Diagnosis of 802.11 Packet Captures: A Multi-Stage Pipeline with Deterministic Reliability Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06871)
*Jerome Henry,Swadhin Pradhan,Miroslav Popovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROBE是一个多阶段流水线，通过确定性PCAP归一化、多候选集成、证据框架和复合可靠性评分，解决了LLM在802.11协议诊断中的幻觉、置信度校准失败和评估偏差问题，在87个企业Wi-Fi捕获上实现0.957加权证据F1和96%自动接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的802.11数据包捕获诊断需要专家协议知识，速度慢、工程师间不一致且不可扩展。基于LLM的方法虽然可行但存在三个关键失败：1) 从捕获中编造协议事件（尤其在截断跟踪中）；2) 产生未校准的置信度分数；3) 当黄金参考由同一模型共同生成时遭受评估偏差。

Method: PROBE采用多阶段流水线：确定性PCAP到文本的归一化（具有帧级可验证性）、多运行多候选集成（可选的跨模型第二意见和渐进混淆）、一个感知判决的证据框架（将缺失失败证据视为贡献证据），以及一个完全确定性的复合可靠性评分（基于证据有效性、运行间稳定性和跨模型一致性，无需LLM自我评估）。

Result: 在87个企业Wi-Fi捕获（104个捕获-审查者配对）上，单次LLM分析加权证据F1从0.871提升到0.912，但在35%的案例中遗漏关键帧。朴素集成投票降至0.842基线以下（多数投票放大保守判决：50%的确认失败被误分类为'无问题'或'证据不足'）。加入证据接地的调和达到0.957 F1，96%自动接受率，最差情况底线超过0.70。LLM自我报告置信度聚类在0.95（71%报告恰好0.95），证实其无信息量。还引入了基于每字段断言匹配的模型无关评估框架，消除了模型共同生成的黄金参考的循环偏差。

Conclusion: PROBE通过证据驱动的多阶段流水线有效解决了LLM在802.11协议诊断中的幻觉、置信度校准和评估偏差问题，显著提升了诊断精度和可靠性，同时提供了具有实际意义的高自动接受率。

Abstract: Diagnosing 802.11 packet captures requires expert protocol knowledge, is slow, inconsistent across engineers, and unscalable. LLM-based approaches sound plausible but fabricate protocol events absent from captures (especially truncated traces), produce uncalibrated confidence scores, and suffer evaluation bias when golden references are co-produced by the model under test. We introduce PROBE (Protocol Reasoning Over evidence-Based Ensembles), a multi-stage pipeline addressing all three failures. It integrates (i) deterministic PCAP-to-text normalization with frame-level verifiability, (ii) multi-run, multi-candidate ensembles with optional cross-model second opinion and progressive obfuscation, (iii) a verdict-aware evidence framework treating absence of failure evidence as contributing evidence, and (iv) a fully deterministic composite reliability score from evidence validity, run-to-run stability, and cross-model agreement without LLM self-assessment.
  On 87 enterprise Wi-Fi captures (104 capture-reviewer pairs), single-pass LLM analysis raises weighted evidence F1 from 0.871 (expert baseline) to 0.912 but misses critical frames in 35% of cases. Naive ensemble voting drops below baseline (0.842) as majority voting amplifies conservative verdicts: 50% of confirmed failures are misclassified as 'no issue' or 'insufficient evidence.' Adding evidence-grounded reconciliation achieves 0.957 F1, a 96% auto-accept rate, and a worst-case floor above 0.70. LLM self-reported confidence clusters at 0.95 regardless of difficulty (71% report exactly 0.95), confirming it is uninformative. We also introduce a model-agnostic evaluation framework using per-field assertion matching, eliminating circular bias from model-co-produced golden references.

</details>


### [225] [GlucoFM-Bench: Benchmarking Time-Series Foundation Models for Blood Glucose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06881)
*Baiying Lu,Zhaohui Liang,Ryan Pontius,Shengpu Tang,Temiloluwa Prioleau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM-Bench是一个综合性基准，用于评估时间序列基础模型(TSFMs)在血糖预测中的表现。在15个糖尿病数据集上测试了8种架构，发现预训练TSFMs在零样本和少样本场景下表现强劲，最佳零样本模型性能与最佳全样本监督模型相差不到5%；但当任务数据充足时，轻量级LSTM在满样本训练下仍优于TSFMs 4-21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血糖预测对糖尿病管理至关重要，但传统机器学习与深度学习模型已被广泛研究，而近期兴起的时间序列基础模型(TSFMs)在这一领域的应用尚未得到系统评估，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建GlucoFM-Bench基准，选取8种代表性架构（预训练TSFMs、时间序列大语言模型、任务特定深度学习模型），在15个公开糖尿病数据集（含1,117名各类糖尿病患者）上，采用零样本、少样本和全样本三种训练协议，系统变化上下文长度和预测视界进行评测。

Result: 预训练TSFMs(特别是Chronos-2和TimesFM)在零样本和少样本迁移上表现优异；最佳零样本模型性能接近最佳全样本监督模型(差距<5%)；但在数据充足时，轻量级LSTM在满样本训练下仍优于TSFMs 4-21%；在1型糖尿病队列及低/高血糖范围存在持续挑战。

Conclusion: GlucoFM-Bench为评估和改进血糖预测基础模型提供了标准化可复现的基准平台，揭示了不同数据条件下的模型选择策略，并强调了超越聚合误差指标进行分层评估的必要性。
